# Threat Model

Jubarte is an in-process Rust Word-document engine for comparison, inspection, edit plans, tracked-change resolution, Markdown conversion, legacy DOC import, validation, scrubbing and PDF/PNG rendering. Native CLI, PyO3/Python and wasm-bindgen/Node/browser surfaces call the shared engine (src/lib.rs:7-35; jubarte-python/Cargo.toml:35-39; jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-32). A separately installed Python MCP server brokers document tools over stdio under one directory capability; the Gemini extension starts this server with workspacePath as its root (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,373-381; gemini-extension.json:5-9). Native CLI self-update is a distinct optional network-and-executable-replacement workflow. Tag releases, local registry publication, CI and external Jules review/remediation are conditional maintainer workflows, not document-runtime capabilities.
Jubarte is a local Rust Word-document toolkit exposed through Rust byte APIs, PyO3 Python, WASM/Node, native and language CLIs, and an optional Python MCP server over stdio. Untrusted DOCX/DOC/Markdown and JSON edit plans feed package admission, XML/legacy parsers, comparisons, edits and PDF/PNG rendering. Native publication and explicit self-update are separate host-authority boundaries (src/lib.rs:7-30,106-174; src/convert/mod.rs:769-798; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-112,373-381). An independent architecture reviewer identified the resource rows below before its execution was interrupted; parent verified their actual consumers offline.
The assigned third validated Standard audit maps the same Rust/native/Python/WASM/Node/MCP engine and ambient font/index resources. Its configuration-to-resource rows are preserved below. Reduction reconciles supplied architecture context only; it adds no source inspection, deployment discovery or validation.

Its native/Python/Node/MCP/WASM authority distinctions, conditional deployment assumptions and reported checkout-versus-artifact-name discrepancy are retained. This serial reducer reconciles supplied context only.

Assigned discovery-0007 supplies the same runtime/maintainer architecture, explicit report confidentiality, public Strict-probe, legacy serialization, inspection-span, comment-anchor and legacy-plan matching boundaries. dedup-0009 reconciles supplied validated artifacts only. Its architecture summary: Jubarte is an in-process Word document engine exposing Rust byte APIs, a native CLI, PyO3/Python APIs and CLI, wasm-bindgen byte APIs and a Node CLI. It reads DOCX/OOXML, Markdown and supported legacy DOC inputs; compares, inspects, edits, resolves revisions, validates/repairs, scrubs and renders documents. Explicitly authorized host adapters publish resulting documents, reports, PDFs and PNGs. A Python MCP adapter serves document tools over stdio under a caller-selected directory capability. Native self-update and maintainer release publication are separate privileged workflows. The independent architecture reviewer reported current HEAD 41019ae16a6123f0273af0ca997901c85437be12; the scan-directory basename was not treated as its revision. Evidence: README.md:12-16,29-42; src/lib.rs:15-35,163-165; jubarte-python/pyproject.toml:42-49; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:373-381; src/update.rs:186-210.

## Assets

- Host process availability and bounded memory/CPU while parsing attacker-authored documents (src/admission.rs:42-105; src/legacy_doc.rs:179-216).
- Integrity/confidentiality of source documents and generated output; edit snapshot SHA-256 and output alias guards (src/bin/jubarte.rs:1610-1667,1768-1826).
- Files outside MCP's held root capability and the operator's installed executable (src/publication.rs:146-200; src/update.rs:198-257,299-321).
- Release/registry authority and signing credentials supplied by the operator or CI secret store, never document contents (.github/workflows/release.yml:261-310; scripts/release.sh:976-1012).
- Document text, comments, authors, revisions, embedded media/fonts and retained OPC parts; derived Markdown, JSON snapshots, edit reports, clean/redline DOCX and PDF/PNG can contain the same confidential material (src/lib.rs:17-29; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-217).
- Host-process memory/CPU availability. Inspection/edit defaults admit 64 MiB compressed, 64 MiB per part, 256 MiB inflated, 10,000 entries and XML depth 256; comparison and rendering use 512 MiB compressed/per part and 2 GiB inflated, with the same entry/depth caps (src/admission.rs:63-99; src/inspect.rs:604-620; src/document_comparer.rs:4926-4927; src/convert/mod.rs:769-778).
- Filesystem authority granted to MCP: the existing expanded/resolved --root directory, default current directory, held through _OutputRoot. Edit artifacts are root/out_dir/{clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff,report.json}, optionally redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png; rendering emits root/out_dir/page-NN.png and optional render.pdf (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:69-108,185-217,234-255,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278).
- Ambient native/Python renderer fonts and persistent font index. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides platform user-font locations; JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides the index, with empty/off disabling it. Defaults resolve to ~/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv on macOS, $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/font-index.tsv or ~/.local/share/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Unix, and %APPDATA%/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Windows. The index is beside, not inside, fonts, and saves via a sibling .tsv.<pid>.tmp file (src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,3090-3107,3182-3229).
- Installed native jubarte executable and GitHub release integrity. The updater consumes jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines releases, SHA256SUMS.txt and the platform archive's jubarte binary; RELEASE_KEYS is currently empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None, so effective authentication is HTTPS plus same-release checksum, not Ed25519 signature verification (src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,198-210,245-257).
- Privileged publication resources: GitHub release write token; optional ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY decoded beneath RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN; local crates.io credentials, npm account/OTP and UV_PUBLISH_TOKEN for PyPI. These have separate provider authority and are used only in release workflows (.github/workflows/release.yml:230-285,297-307,356-369; scripts/release.sh:374-380,919,934-956,1069).
- CI runner workspace/cache integrity and external-review credentials. Standard CI runs PR code on self-hosted runners with contents:read and nonpersistent checkout credentials. External Jules review separately receives JULES_API_KEY and a GitHub token with pull-request/check write permissions; companion remediation receives JULES_API_KEY under a trusted-user/event gate (.github/workflows/ci.yml:13-46; .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:20-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:35-54,261-272,320-324).
- Confidential document text, comments, revisions, embedded media and resulting DOCX/PDF/PNG/report artifacts. MCP edit output includes clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff and report.json beneath the selected out_dir: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:195.
- Host filesystem contents and existing outputs. MCP grants a directory capability once; CLI publication explicitly grants ambient local access: src/publication.rs:155; src/publication.rs:162; src/publication.rs:175; src/publication.rs:211.
- Host memory and CPU handling untrusted packages. Default admission caps compressed bytes at 64 MiB, entries at 10,000, individual inflated parts at 64 MiB, total inflation at 256 MiB and XML depth at 256. Comparison defaults are materially larger: 512 MiB compressed/per-part and 2 GiB total: src/admission.rs:63; src/admission.rs:87.
- Installed executable integrity, GitHub release artifacts, release signing authority and package publication authority: src/update.rs:198; src/update.rs:245; .github/workflows/release.yml:263; .github/workflows/release.yml:358; scripts/release.sh:919; scripts/release.sh:935; scripts/release.sh:952; scripts/release.sh:1069.
- Native renderer font files and persistent index. Font search uses configured/platform directories; default index is beside the fonts directory, not inside it, and records font file paths consumed on later conversions: src/convert/font.rs:2637; src/convert/font.rs:3100; src/convert/font.rs:3165.
- Confidential source DOCX, legacy DOC and Markdown content; revision authors, comments, metadata, media, generated agent views, reports, patches, clean documents and redlines (src/lib.rs:15-30; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-217). MCP edit exports have distinct derived destinations under the selected root and out_dir: clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff, report.json, optionally redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png.
- Integrity of edit targets and their source revision. EditPlan.source_sha256 is optional, and when supplied the actual source hash must match or STALE_SOURCE is returned; author labels are document data rather than authenticated identities (src/edit.rs:97-108,2081-2092).
- Host filesystem content reachable with the invoking OS account; MCP's selected root directory capability; CLI Markdown resource roots; ambient native renderer font directories; output staging and existing output bytes (src/publication.rs:153-199,203-225,421-505; src/bin/jubarte.rs:2858-2871,2937-2943; src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652).
- Availability of the embedding Python process, native process or browser/Node WASM instance. Ordinary admission defaults allow 64 MiB compressed/per-part, 256 MiB inflated total, 10,000 entries and XML depth 256; comparison defaults instead allow 512 MiB compressed/per-part and 2 GiB inflated total (src/admission.rs:63-100).
- Installed jubarte executable integrity and the authority to replace it. Current trusted release-key configuration is empty, so the updater uses release checksums rather than mandatory signatures (src/update.rs:44-48,198-210,245-257).
- Release signing secret reference ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, GitHub release publication token reference GITHUB_TOKEN, local PyPI token reference UV_PUBLISH_TOKEN and local npm/Cargo publishing credentials. These are separate capabilities and are not document-processing credentials (.github/workflows/release.yml:230-275,278-307,356-369; scripts/release.sh:919,934-954,1069).
- GitHub review comments/checks and external Jules session capability. Ordinary reviewer and companion-remediation workflows receive different permissions and apply different caller gates (.github/workflows/jules-review.yml:19-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:35-52,259-272,320-324).
- Confidential document text, revision history, comments, embedded media and derived PDF/PNG/Markdown/diff/report outputs; MCP tools return document text, findings and reports as well as output paths to their connected client (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:131-150,185-217,234-255,354-368).
- Host filesystem integrity and intended output destinations. Native publication stages bytes beneath each selected output parent; MCP publication instead retains the operator-granted OutputRoot directory handle and accepts only portable relative output names (src/publication.rs:146-225,228-270,420-505).
- Source-to-edit binding and the integrity of checked primary output. V1 source SHA-256 checking is conditional on the optional plan field; V2 requires source.sha256 equality. Prepared edits retain source, normalized request and actual chosen-primary digests (src/edit.rs:2081-2089, src/edit/v2_apply.rs:84-99, src/edit/prepared.rs:157-181).
- Process availability and memory during ZIP/XML, legacy document, font, image and rendering work. Agent admission defaults are 64 MiB compressed/per-part, 256 MiB total inflated, 10,000 entries and XML depth 256; comparison defaults are 512 MiB compressed/per-part and 2 GiB total inflated with the same entry/depth caps. Overrides replace individual values (src/admission.rs:63-99,103-165).
- Native font sources and font-index integrity: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR or platform data folders supply fonts; JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX or the parent of the font folder supplies font-index.tsv. Cached face paths are read by the renderer and the index is persisted through a sibling temporary file and rename (src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,3098-3107,3154-3172,3213-3229).
- Installed executable authenticity and release publication authority. Current self-update trusts GitHub release delivery plus SHA256SUMS.txt; RELEASE_KEYS is empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None. CI signing secrets, GitHub contents-write credentials and local package-registry credentials are separate authorities (src/update.rs:25-48,198-210,245-257; .github/workflows/release.yml:230-264,278-308,356-369; scripts/release.sh:904-919,929-958,1063-1069).
- Confidential document bytes, revisions/comments/authors and derived renderings/reports; integrity of selected input/output files and admitted package structures (src/lib.rs:17-30; src/publication.rs:146-225).
- MCP root filesystem capability; native executable integrity on self-update; release publishing/signing authority (.github/workflows/release.yml:230-275,356-369; src/update.rs:25-48,198-258).
- Native renderers read installed/user/Word cloud-cache fonts and persist a per-user font index, distinct from document output-root authority (src/convert/font.rs:2228-2236,2633-2652,3090-3107,3213-3227).
- MCP edit outputs are root/out_dir/{clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff,report.json}, optional redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png, available to authorized root filesystem readers and reported to attached client; native OutputRoot publishes with default no overwrite and per-file atomicity (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:153-217; src/publication.rs:168-199).
- Confidential input documents, document text, comments, authorship and revision history; derived clean/redline DOCX files, Markdown patches, JSON reports and rendered PDF/PNG artifacts. MCP edit writes <root>/<out_dir>/clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff and report.json, with optional redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:152-205.
- Integrity of caller-selected local files and the MCP server's granted document directory. The directory capability is held open, while ambient CLI publication intentionally accepts local paths outside any common root. Evidence: src/publication.rs:146-217.
- Host memory and CPU used by archive decompression, XML parsing, document comparison, legacy DOC parsing, fonts, image decoding and rendering. Default admission limits are 64 MiB compressed, 10,000 entries, 64 MiB per part, 256 MiB inflated and XML depth 256; comparison limits raise compressed/per-part bounds to 512 MiB and aggregate inflated bytes to 2 GiB. Evidence: src/admission.rs:39-100.
- Native renderer font files and persistent index metadata. The index defaults to ~/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv on macOS, $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/font-index.tsv or ~/.local/share/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Linux, and %APPDATA%\jubarte\font-index.tsv on Windows; JUBARTE_FONT_DIR changes its default parent and JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides or disables it. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652, src/convert/font.rs:3098-3107, src/convert/font.rs:3182-3229.
- Installed CLI executable, release archives/checksums, package-registry identities and publication credentials. Current updater trusted signing keys are empty and the first signed release is unset. Evidence: src/update.rs:25-48, src/update.rs:198-258, scripts/release.sh:374-380, scripts/release.sh:919-959, scripts/release.sh:1063-1069.
- GitHub release-write authority, optional release signing private key, Jules API credentials and delegated review/remediation capabilities. These are privileged development workflows, separate from document processing. Evidence: .github/workflows/release.yml:230-307, .github/workflows/release.yml:356-369, .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:17-41, .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:259-324.
- Confidential document text, revisions, comments, metadata, embedded media and generated DOCX/PDF/PNG/report artifacts; these are returned to the invoking caller or written to its selected destinations (src/lib.rs:17-29; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:195-217).
- Host filesystem authority: native CLI and ordinary Python/npm adapters possess ambient process access; the MCP document I/O capability is an opened root directory (src/publication.rs:146-165,203-225; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:126-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:106-111).
- Process availability and memory across Rust, Python and WASM consumers. Default admission allows 64 MiB compressed/per part, 256 MiB total inflated, 10,000 entries and depth 256; comparison allows 512 MiB compressed/per part and 2 GiB total, with the same entry/depth limits (src/admission.rs:63-100).
- Installed executable integrity, release archives/checksums and conditional signing authority (src/update.rs:25-48,198-257; .github/workflows/release.yml:261-309).
- Native font files and persistent font-index.tsv, including cached font paths and temporary index files. Their authority follows host environment and filesystem permissions, independently of the MCP document root (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3095-3107,3156-3165,3213-3229).
- Maintainer GitHub release token, conditional ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, registry publication credentials and package integrity (.github/workflows/release.yml:234-235,261-309,356-369; scripts/release.sh:919-959,1063-1069).
- Confidential document text, revisions, comments, authors, images, embedded fonts and derived Markdown/PDF/PNG/reports; MCP returns text, inspection data, reports and rendered page text to its connected caller (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:132-150,194-218,246-255).
- Integrity and recoverability of caller-selected documents and output files. Native ambient publication stages and verifies bytes before per-file installation; capability-rooted publication additionally confines relative paths (src/publication.rs:161-225,535-570).
- Host availability: package inflation, XML parsing, comparison, image decoding and font parsing consume caller process resources. Default admission is 64 MiB compressed/per-part, 256 MiB aggregate inflated, 10,000 entries and depth 256; comparison/rendering uses 512 MiB compressed/per-part and 2 GiB aggregate with the same entry/depth caps (src/admission.rs:63-99; src/convert/mod.rs:773-778).
- Native font source integrity and persistent font index. Its configured location may be outside the document root and contains font source paths and filesystem stamps (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3090-3107,3154-3170,3183-3229).
- Installed native executable integrity and release distribution authority. Updater selects jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines and SHA256SUMS.txt; signing trust keys are currently empty and the signed-release floor is None (src/update.rs:25-48,198-257).
- Release credentials: GitHub Actions GITHUB_TOKEN, optional ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, and operator registry credentials/OTP. Their authority is publication/signing, independent of ordinary document processing (.github/workflows/release.yml:230-235,261-307,356-369; scripts/release.sh:934-958).
- Shared self-hosted CI filesystem/process authority and private build-cache connectivity, distinct from GitHub token permission (.github/actions/mbx/action.yml:8-32; .github/workflows/ci.yml:26-48).
- Confidential document bytes and extracted text, including paragraphs, comments, revisions, metadata, embedded images/fonts and relationship targets; generated clean/redline DOCX, rendered PDF/PNG and edit/audit reports. MCP intentionally returns text through read tools and page text through rendering, while document/image bytes are published as files. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:132-150,185-217,229-255; docs/adoption/mcp.md:69-73.
- Integrity of edited documents, source snapshots and revision attribution. SHA-256 protects optional edit-plan snapshot binding; paragraph/text anchors and plan preparation protect edit applicability. Author strings are supplied document metadata, not authenticated identities. Evidence: src/inspect.rs:273-277; src/edit.rs:1744-1761,2073-2105; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:258-276.
- Filesystem authority granted to the MCP server: explicit input reads and output publication beneath resolved --root, defaulting to startup working directory. The persistent native OutputRoot holds a cap-std directory handle. This is distinct from ambient filesystem authority used by CLI adapters and renderer font/cache operations. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1277; src/publication.rs:146-199,203-225.
- Renderer resources outside explicit document paths: installed font files, Word cloud fonts and a persistent font-index TSV containing font source paths, file stamps and directory stamps. Default index location is the parent of Jubarte's font directory plus font-index.tsv, not a child of fonts/. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2258-2315,2633-2652,3090-3106,3154-3169,3192-3227.
- Process memory, CPU and availability across native, Python and WASM callers. Default package admission allows 64 MiB compressed and per part, 256 MiB inflated, 10,000 entries and XML depth 256; comparison uses 512 MiB compressed/per part and 2 GiB inflated with the same entry/depth limits. XML limits default to 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte attribute names, 64 MiB values and 128 MiB start tags. These are configured objectives, with actual enforcement findings recorded separately. Evidence: src/admission.rs:63-100; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:49-57.

These belong to separate workflows and recipients; their actual grants and runner isolation are external deployment facts. Evidence: .github/workflows/release.yml:230-263,278-308,356-369; scripts/release.sh:919,931-956,1069; .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:19-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:46-54,259-324.
- Confidential document content, embedded media, revisions, comments, authors, metadata, rendered output, and edit reports entrusted to the processing host and intentionally returned or exported by its selected interface. These are not encrypted by the engine. Sources: src/lib.rs:17-30, jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-217, SECURITY.md:79-85.
- Integrity of document edits and correspondence between a plan and its intended input snapshot. The legacy edit path verifies source_sha256 when provided; this guard is optional and recorded as guarded in its report. Sources: src/edit.rs:2081-2088, src/edit.rs:2480-2482.
- Filesystem authority granted to MCP: canonical root and paths beneath it, including out_dir/clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff, report.json, optional redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png; render exports use out_dir/page-NN.png and render.pdf. Sources: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:69-108,185-217,234-255.
- Host filesystem integrity outside intentionally authorized CLI destinations and Markdown image resource roots. Native CLI publication uses ambient destination-parent authority; MCP uses a held root capability. Sources: src/publication.rs:146-165,175-225, src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273,2858-2871.
- Native renderer fonts and persistent index. Defaults: macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv; Linux $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/font-index.tsv or $HOME/.local/share/jubarte/font-index.tsv; Windows $APPDATA\jubarte\font-index.tsv. Environment overrides change locations. Sources: src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,3090-3107,3182-3229.
- Embedding-process availability and CPU/memory. Default admission permits 64 MiB compressed/per-part and 256 MiB total inflated; compare permits 512 MiB compressed/per-part and 2 GiB inflated. Both retain 10,000 entries and XML depth 256. Sources: src/admission.rs:63-99.
- Installed executable integrity and release credentials. Current updater trusts GitHub transport plus SHA256SUMS.txt, with no compiled signing key/threshold. Release signing uses ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY reference; GitHub uses GITHUB_TOKEN, local release also uses registry credentials. Sources: src/update.rs:25-48,198-257, .github/workflows/release.yml:261-309,356-369, scripts/release.sh:370-380,919-959,1069.
- Privacy of content intended for redaction: edit postconditions refuse retained copies through scrub::leaks (src/edit.rs:4724-4748; src/edit/prepared.rs:798-804,873). UTF-16 XML support and untouched-part preservation create a relevant encoding boundary (src/admission.rs:768-781; src/edit.rs:5381-5384).

## Trust Boundaries

- Document author -> Rust parsing/rendering -> caller process. admission bounds compressed bytes, entry counts, per-part/total inflation and selected XML depth/tag policy; downstream XML, numeric layout, images/fonts and legacy fields require their own effective controls (src/admission.rs:442-580,788-827; src/convert/mod.rs:769-798).
- Python/Node/native CLI callers choose ambient local input/output paths; these callers already possess operator file authority. MCP clients instead provide relative document/output names beneath --root, default current working directory; build_server resolves that root once and _OutputRoot retains native cap_std::Dir authority for actual I/O (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-112,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1279; src/publication.rs:146-200).
- MCP/native rendering -> ambient font resources: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides the platform user font directory; macOS ~/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts, Unix $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts or ~/.local/share/jubarte/fonts, Windows %APPDATA%/jubarte/fonts. Fonts are read by conversion outside the document root (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652).
- Rendering -> persistent font index: JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX selects a path; empty/off disables it; default parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv. FONT_INDEX loads that path and save merges and writes path.with_extension(tsv.<pid>.tmp), then renames it to the index (src/convert/font.rs:3100-3107,3213-3229). This is component ambient authority, distinct from tool document/output confinement.
- Explicit self-update -> fixed jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines GitHub release and SHA256SUMS.txt -> extracted platform executable -> installed binary. TLS/checksum verification, optional compiled version-gated signing policy, and lstat regular-file hook enforce distinct steps (src/update.rs:25-48,198-257,274-285,299-321; Cargo.toml:137-148).
- Privileged release workflows: release.yml optionally signs with secret-store ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, computes SHA256SUMS.txt and publishes GitHub assets. Operator scripts/release.sh:976-1012 can recover workflow artifacts into dist/release, compute checksums and create a release without that signing step.
- Caller-supplied DOCX/ZIP/XML/media/fonts cross into the host engine. Admission counts actual inflation, checks package identity, names and XML limits; production archive opening rejects header/extent/special-entry defects. Low-level permissive parse_xdocument does not apply input policy: callers of raw XML APIs must select checked parsing or prevalidate. The deliberate debug archive reader is an inspection exception (src/admission.rs:13-28,439-566; src/opc/archive.rs:20-51; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:21-36,98-135; SECURITY.md:132-139).
- Legacy DOC is a separate OLE/FIB/piece-table parser, not a ZIP-admission path. It converts the supported main story to Markdown/DOCX and explicitly excludes encrypted/obfuscated and older Word formats (src/legacy_doc.rs:13-50).
- Edit-plan author transfers requested mutations and optionally source identity to the engine. Schema version and a supplied source_sha256 are checked; the v1 source guard is optional, not an authorization or mandatory freshness credential (src/edit.rs:97-106,2072-2094). Filesystem publication is a separate boundary from in-memory edit acceptance.
- Native CLI uses ambient caller-selected output paths. publish_paths holds each parent directory, stages into parent/.jubarte-<uuid>.partial/bytes, uses Unix staging mode 0700, and commits per file; OutputRoot additionally validates portable relative names and confines capability-relative I/O. Multi-file publication is explicitly not atomic across the set, and errors record already committed files (src/publication.rs:83-143,146-225,228-273,420-505). Python and npm general-output helpers instead directly call Path.write_bytes/fs.writeFileSync; native publication controls cannot be assumed for those helpers (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-113,126-132; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:76-79,106-110).
- MCP caller gains document read/write operations only within the operator-selected root. Python resolve-based checks are early diagnostics; native cap-std OutputRoot owns the actual read/write confinement. overwrite is caller-controlled, false by default; annotations are hints, not per-tool authorization. stdio possession is the transport access boundary; no distinct users/tenants are established by this adapter (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,152-160,373-381; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- Native Markdown image references cross from document content into local reads. Effective resource root is --resource-path or the Markdown file's parent, falling back to current directory. Percent-decoded targets must canonically remain under that root and actual reads use its held OutputRoot capability; URL schemes are refused. The library's image callback is separately supplied by the embedding caller, while Python/npm CLI reject resource_path support (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2870,2936-2943,3365-3400; src/markdown/mod.rs:196-198; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:1057-1064; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:724-727).
- Native/Python rendering may read installed fonts and update the ambient per-user/configured index even when invoked through MCP. MCP's document-path capability is not a whole-process filesystem sandbox. Browser/WASM uses embedded/bundled faces rather than installed fonts; its byte APIs do not expose native OutputRoot publication (src/convert/font.rs:2258-2285,2633-2652,3095-3107,3182-3229; README.md:901-920; Cargo.toml:149-152).
- Self-update crosses from GitHub release service/assets into executable replacement. Noninteractive operation requires --check or --yes; a terminal otherwise confirms replacement. Default selection installs newer versions; explicit --version permits downgrade. Unix archive member is jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte, Windows member ends jubarte.exe; an extracted regular-file verification hook runs before the delegated swap. Current release keys are empty, so signature support is compiled but not enforcing (src/update.rs:91-124,144-160,186-257,307-320).
- Tag-triggered release builders have contents:read; the separate release job has contents:write. Archives/wheels are downloaded into dist, optional signing rewrites archives before SHA256SUMS.txt generation, then gh publishes dist/* to the tag. The signing private key is step-scoped and decoded into a temporary directory removed by EXIT trap (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-89,230-285,297-307,317-322,356-369).
- Local scripts/release.sh uses the operator's separate registry credentials, validates tool/auth availability, verifies the tagged engine stamp and prompts for the exact version unless YES bypass applies. It also executes an external app checkout's scripts/release-engine.sh selected by JUBARTE_APP_DIR or jubarte-app; that external implementation and its downstream authority are absent from this repository (scripts/release.sh:374-389,870-899,919,934-956,1069).
- Ordinary PR CI crosses into self-hosted execution with contents:read and persist-credentials:false; those settings govern GitHub-token persistence/permissions, not runner filesystem isolation. Jules review and companion remediation are independent grants: reviewer action receives PR/check-write GitHub token and Jules key, with enable_approve:false; remediation permits only configured trusted author/dispatcher/event combinations, then requests AUTO_CREATE_PR with require_plan_approval:false and starting_branch equal to mutable PR head_ref. The static analysis checkout is bound to head_sha, but the external session input is a branch name (.github/workflows/ci.yml:13-46; .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:20-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:74-102,261-272,320-324).
- Gemini extension startup passes --from jubarte-redlines[mcp] without a version pin to uvx; package resolution is host/package-manager authority before MCP opens workspacePath as root (gemini-extension.json:5-9).
- Untrusted document suppliers cross into in-process ZIP/XML/media parsing. Admission applies configured byte/count/depth budgets and bounded inflated reads; shared archive opening applies zip_defects before use. Direct unchecked archive opening is retained for diagnostic inspection: src/admission.rs:439; src/admission.rs:531; src/opc/archive.rs:20; src/opc/archive.rs:42.
- Public checked XML parsing validates before DOM construction; permissive parse_xdocument remains a different API whose caller must establish appropriate trust/admission. Tag defaults include 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte qualified attribute names, 64 MiB attribute values and 128 MiB start tags: src/xmllinq/parse.rs:49; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:119; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:134.
- MCP caller arguments cross into host document reads and publication. Python resolves paths for early diagnostics, while native _OutputRoot performs capability-relative read/publish using the directory held by publication::OutputRoot. Tool annotations are hints to the host, not an approval or per-tool authorization mechanism: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:72; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:75; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:84; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:101; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1254; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1260; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1265; docs/adoption/mcp.md:39.
- Native CLI and Python CLI callers already possess ambient local path authority. CLI publication uses publish_paths; Python CLI reads caller-specified paths. Their authority must not be conflated with the MCP root capability: src/publication.rs:211; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:61; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:73.
- Rendering crosses from document-requested font families into ambient platform/configured font resources and cache writes. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR controls the font folder; JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX independently overrides or disables the index; absent override, the index is parent(font-folder)/font-index.tsv. These operations use std::fs rather than MCP OutputRoot: src/convert/font.rs:2637; src/convert/font.rs:3100; src/convert/font.rs:3165; src/convert/font.rs:3213.
- Explicit updater crosses from GitHub release authority into installed executable replacement. It selects a platform-specific archive/member, requests SHA256SUMS.txt verification and checks the extracted binary is a regular file. Signature verification is conditional, and this source has RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None: src/update.rs:25; src/update.rs:44; src/update.rs:48; src/update.rs:91; src/update.rs:104; src/update.rs:198; src/update.rs:245; src/update.rs:308.
- Tag-triggered CI builds cross into a privileged release job with contents:write. Only the signing step receives ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, which is decoded into ephemeral runner files; the publication step receives GITHUB_TOKEN and uploads dist/* to the current repository release: .github/workflows/release.yml:15; .github/workflows/release.yml:20; .github/workflows/release.yml:230; .github/workflows/release.yml:234; .github/workflows/release.yml:263; .github/workflows/release.yml:278; .github/workflows/release.yml:285; .github/workflows/release.yml:358; .github/workflows/release.yml:364.
- npm edit plan recording has its own basename/PID partial-file publication path (jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:408-425,442-445). It uses ambient Node filesystem authority and does not inherit native Rust private-staging controls. Intentional resolution of the caller-selected plan symlink is distinct from an attacker-selected staging symlink.
- Redaction and metadata scrub cross from document-retained parts into an asserted outgoing privacy postcondition. BOM UTF-16 auxiliary XML is admitted/preserved (src/admission.rs:764-780; src/opc/mod.rs:368-376,659-664), while leak checking must interpret that same representation (src/scrub.rs:467-475; src/edit.rs:4727-4747). Property discovery must follow package relationship targets rather than only fixed conventional paths (src/scrub.rs:39-43,357-384).
- Untrusted package bytes cross into ZIP/OPC/XML parsing and rendering. Admission prechecks budgets, checks duplicate entries and shared ZIP structural defects, and bounds actual inflated reads rather than trusting declared sizes (src/admission.rs:439-470,531-535). The shared checked ZIP opener rejects defects; debug is an intentional unchecked diagnostic exception (src/opc/archive.rs:15-19,34-51). Checked XML APIs and raw permissive DOM APIs are separate caller contracts, explicitly distinguished by SECURITY.md:51-65.
- Untrusted legacy DOC is a separate OLE/FIB/piece-table parsing path, subsequently converted into Markdown/DOCX; it is not covered merely by identifying DOCX ZIP admission (src/legacy_doc.rs:13-50). Embedded fonts, image decoders and layout are additional native parser surfaces identified by Cargo.toml:107-110.
- Native CLI operators select ambient input/output paths. src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273 invokes publication::publish_paths, whose root=None backend opens output parents with ambient authority (src/publication.rs:203-225,431-446). Outputs stage beside their destination in .jubarte-<UUID>.partial/bytes, with Unix staging directory mode 0700 and exclusive creation (src/publication.rs:472-505). Publication stages all files first but commits per file; failures can leave named committed outputs, and Windows directory synchronization is intentionally a no-op (src/publication.rs:100-130,168-174,387-405). This is not an all-files transaction.
- Python and Node CLI callers retain ambient local filesystem authority. Python's _read_side consumes caller paths and _write calls Path.write_bytes; Node's read and write helpers call fs.readFileSync/writeFileSync (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:73-83,126-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-56,106-111). Native staged-publication properties cannot be assumed for these helpers.
- The MCP operator grants a directory root once; a connected stdio client can invoke all registered read/write tools within that grant. --root defaults to current working directory, is expanded/resolved, and must be a directory; no HTTP listener or application authentication is configured in this entrypoint (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:373-381). Python containment checks are early diagnostics; actual reads and publications use _native._OutputRoot, backed by cap_std directory authority and portable relative-write validation (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:69-108; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:153-199,228-273,431-463). overwrite is a per-call replacement capability, defaulting false. ToolAnnotations are metadata, not a separate authorization layer (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,153-160).
- CLI Markdown image targets cross into local filesystem reads only through a selected resource root: explicit --resource-path wins, otherwise the Markdown file's parent, otherwise '.' (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2937-2943). The loader canonicalizes and holds that directory capability, applies image policy and reads relative to the capability; URLs are not fetched by this loader (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2851-2877). This capability is separate from output publication authority.
- Native renderer font discovery is ambient OS-account access, independent of MCP's document/output root. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides per-user locations; absent it, Windows uses APPDATA/jubarte/fonts, macOS HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts, and other native platforms XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts or HOME/.local/share/jubarte/fonts (src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652). Native discovery also examines named system/Word font directories and HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts/<family>; the document-supplied family must be a single normal path component for that derived cloud directory (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2281-2309).
- Browser/Node WASM callers pass byte arrays and JSON options into the engine. The bindings disable default engine features, omit native publication on wasm32 and support comparison-budget overrides; the binding explicitly warns that its 2 GiB inflated default can exceed practical WASM heap capacity (jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-23; src/lib.rs:163-165; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:63-97). Hosting applications own admission tuning, upload authorization, origin policy, worker isolation and persistence.
- Explicit jubarte self-update crosses from GitHub release metadata/assets into executable replacement. Repo is jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines; the chosen archive member is jubarte-<version>-<platform>/jubarte[.exe]; SHA256SUMS.txt is configured; extracted binary must lstat as a regular file (src/update.rs:25-27,91-109,198-210,299-321). Without --check/--yes, noninteractive runs stop before lookup; installation requires confirmation unless --yes; explicitly selected versions may downgrade (src/update.rs:120-125,144-160,234-247). Current RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None mean signature enforcement is inactive (src/update.rs:44-48,274-285).
- Tag-driven GitHub release automation builds platform archives/wheels with read-only job permissions, then a separate contents:write job signs when configured, computes checksums and publishes dist/* to the triggering tag's release (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-89,230-275,295-321,356-369). The signing secret is scoped to the signing step, decoded into RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN and removed by an EXIT trap; the signing tool and runner are recipients of this authority (.github/workflows/release.yml:261-307).
- The local release script separately publishes the Rust package, both npm packages and PyPI artifacts. Typed version confirmation precedes pushing unless YES is set, then each registry operation uses its own tool/account authority (scripts/release.sh:877-919,929-959,1063-1069). The script calls a downstream app checkout, but that application's implementation is absent from this repository and is not included as inspected architecture (scripts/release.sh:141-175).
- PR automation crosses repository-controlled PR source, descriptions and tool output into external actions and agent sessions. The normal review workflow excludes fork/Dependabot PRs and grants contents:read, pull-requests:write and checks:write; companion remediation additionally gates session creation on TRUSTED_USERS=['arthrod'] and selected events, uses AUTO_CREATE_PR and require_plan_approval=false (.github/workflows/jules-review.yml:19-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:35-52,259-272,320-324). External action internals, Jules integration permissions and runtime restrictions are not present here.
- CI/docs workflows execute checked-out repository code on self-hosted runners for pull_request events. Workflow token permissions are contents:read and checkout disables persisted credentials, but host filesystem, network, persistent-state isolation and runner admission controls are external deployment controls (.github/workflows/ci.yml:13-40,72-99; .github/workflows/docs.yml:42-86,98-99).
- Validated audit refinements: package admission's extension-based selection does not cover relationship-resolved notes with non-XML suffixes (src/admission.rs:533-570; src/document_comparer.rs:6318-6328,6461-6479). Admission also ignores DTD while the raw DOM disagrees on declaration boundaries (src/admission.rs:798-820; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:539-547,660-662). These are findings, not assertions that the intended policy is fully enforced.
- Untrusted DOCX/ZIP/XML bytes cross into package admission and then DOM/document operations. Opened::open admits using default limits, normalizes Strict packages and opens/parses within those limits. General rendering calls admit_package before package/DOM construction. The common archive opener rejects reported structural defects, while the deliberately unchecked opener supports triage and admission's own typed checks (src/inspect.rs:604-622; src/convert/mod.rs:769-798; src/opc/archive.rs:15-51).
- Raw XML library callers choose between checked and permissive APIs. try_parse_xdocument_with_limits validates before constructing the DOM; parse_xdocument explicitly requires a prior gate and does not enforce input policy itself. Shared defaults include 1,024 element attributes, 1,024 attribute-name bytes, 64 MiB attribute values and 128 MiB start tags (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:25-36,48-57,91-98,119-135).
- Edit requests cross from caller-authored plans to document mutations. V1 checks a supplied source hash, refuses blank authors and signed/macro-bearing inputs; V2 requires the exact source hash and resolves scope/context before operations. These are document-consistency controls, not user authentication or authorization (src/edit.rs:2072-2105; src/edit/v2_apply.rs:84-110). Dry run and application share in-memory preparation; filesystem publication remains a separate host operation (src/edit/prepared.rs:5-13,157-186).
- Native CLI users grant ambient authority through selected input/output paths. publish_paths opens and retains each actual parent rather than enforcing a global document root; staging uses an exclusive UUID directory and file, with Unix staging directory mode 0700. Publication is atomic per file and records partial commits/durability uncertainty rather than promising set-wide rollback (src/publication.rs:5-7,83-143,203-225,420-505).
- The MCP connected client can invoke registered read and write tools within the root chosen by the server operator. Startup defaults --root to cwd, resolves it once and serves stdio. Python containment checks are preliminary diagnostics; actual document reads and output publication pass through native _OutputRoot and cap-std directory-relative operations. No per-tool user identity or tenant separation is established; tool annotations are hints, while OutputRoot owns filesystem confinement (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1276; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- Native font discovery/cache is a separate authority from document-root publication. Font-family lookup can inspect system, Word, cloud and configured user-font directories; cloud family names must be one normal path component. The index may read cached face paths and write its configured file outside the MCP root. Its enforcing boundary is host environment/filesystem authority, not OutputRoot (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2263-2315,2633-2652,2855-2862,3098-3107,3154-3172,3213-3229).
- WASM byte APIs do not receive native publication::OutputRoot because that module is compiled only outside wasm32. Python and WASM bindings disable engine default features and request only cli grammar, so optional self-update is not part of those engine dependencies. Node's separately shipped CLI grants ambient filesystem access through node:fs around the WASM engine; its read/write helpers are distinct from native staged publication (src/lib.rs:147-149,163-165; jubarte-python/Cargo.toml:35-39; jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-23; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-79,106-111).
- Self-update is an explicit native CLI capability. It selects fixed jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines repository, platform archive and inner binary path; requests checksum verification and regular-file verification; prompts unless --yes, and --check does not install. Current keys leave signature verification disabled. Dependency-owned download, archive extraction and executable-swap behavior is not established merely by this wrapper (src/bin/jubarte.rs:3892-3896,4062-4073; src/update.rs:25-48,186-257,274-285,299-321).
- Release publication is privileged operator/CI work, separate from document processing. CI triggers on v* tags, checks tag/package version, grants build jobs contents:read and release job contents:write, optionally signs assets with step-scoped ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, then publishes dist/* using step-scoped GH_TOKEN. Local release script has packaging dry runs and version confirmation unless YES, then separately publishes crates.io, two npm packages and PyPI with configured credentials (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,31-49,230-264,278-308,317-322,356-369; scripts/release.sh:729-747,874-888,904-919,929-958,1039-1069).
- Untrusted DOCX/ZIP/XML, legacy DOC, embedded images/fonts and edit plans enter the engine. Default admission bounds archive/part bytes 64 MiB, 10000 entries, inflation 256 MiB, XML depth 256; comparison allows 512 MiB archive/part and 2 GiB inflation. Permissive parse_xdocument requires a prior gate (src/admission.rs:15-28,63-99; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:21-36).
- MCP stdio client invokes document tools under operator --root (default cwd). Python resolves paths for diagnostics; native _OutputRoot owns confined reads/publication through a held capability. Annotations are hints, not caller authorization (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1277; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- Native CLI grants ambient paths with staged per-file publication. Python/Node CLI ambient reads/writes are separate from MCP confinement (src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; src/publication.rs:203-225; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/document.py:142-145; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:126-132; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-56,106-110).
- Native Markdown images use --resource-path or Markdown parent directory; URL schemes/canonical escape targets are refused, followed by capability-relative read (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2835-2876,2936-2943).
- Self-update enters executable replacement only on explicit command and confirmation/--yes. RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None mean checksum-only today; optional Ed25519 support is inactive (src/update.rs:25-48,120-125,198-258,274-285; docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:59-82).
- Tag-triggered release builds are read-only; release job has contents:write, signing step receives signing secret and publishes dist/* with workflow token. These are CI/maintainer authorities (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-55,230-275,356-369).
- Native rendering, including MCP rendering, has separate ambient font discovery/cache I/O: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides user font roots; JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX controls index persistence independently of document-root grants (src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,3098-3107,3192-3229).
- PR workflows run checked-out code on self-hosted runners sharing the private mbx cache http://mbx-cache:7543, namespace jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines. Comments promise protected-push publishing and PR reads, but server writer authorization and object validation are absent from this repository (.github/workflows/ci.yml:13-17,34-48,72-101; .github/actions/mbx/action.yml:8-22).
- Outgoing privacy certification has independent controls: the leaks XML decoder must honor supported UTF-16, matching business XML attributes must not inherit namespace-blind numeric exemptions, metadata discovery must follow actual property targets, and Part::load must consistently decode selected property XML. A fix to one does not subsume the others (src/scrub.rs:188-199,357-385,467-475,484-490,522-552; src/opc/mod.rs:560-564).
- Eager embedded-font loading copies and retains each unknown alias/style even when runs do not use it. Resource accounting must distinguish stored bytes from decoded buffers and alias multiplicity; Arc container values do not imply resolved-part sharing (src/convert/mod.rs:806-809; src/convert/font_table.rs:177-193,205-231).
- Document producer -> Rust package/XML consumers: caller-controlled ZIP records, part names, compressed data and XML cross into trusted process memory. Admission checks central/local ZIP consistency, special-entry defects, case-folded duplicate names, encryption/compression policy, streamed inflated-byte budgets and XML checks. InputLimitOverrides rejects unknown configuration fields. Evidence: src/admission.rs:103-138, src/admission.rs:439-553, src/opc/archive.rs:34-51. The library forbids unsafe Rust in its own code: src/lib.rs:86.
- Library caller -> raw XML parser: parse_xdocument is intentionally permissive and allocates a character vector without applying input policy; try_parse_xdocument_with_limits validates before constructing its DOM. Callers choosing the raw API own prior admission and process limits. Evidence: src/xmllinq/parse.rs:88-135. Default tag limits are 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte qualified attribute names, 64 MiB attribute values and 128 MiB start tags: src/xmllinq/parse.rs:48-57.
- Legacy DOC producer -> legacy parser: supported conversion follows the separate compound-file/WordDocument/FIB/table pipeline rather than DOCX ZIP admission. Stream structure and encryption checks belong to legacy_doc, and process resource limits remain a host obligation. Evidence: src/legacy_doc.rs:174-216, src/legacy_doc.rs:765-795.
- Edit-plan producer -> document transaction: version-1 source SHA-256 is checked when supplied; version-2 source SHA-256 is mandatory and checked after plan validation. Version-1 editing refuses packages containing XML signatures or vbaProject.bin. These integrity checks bind source bytes, not authenticated identity or human approval. Evidence: src/edit.rs:2072-2104, src/edit/v2_apply.rs:75-100.
- MCP client -> document filesystem: all connected clients receive the tools installed by build_server, with document reads/publication mediated by _native._OutputRoot and cap_std::fs::Dir. --root defaults to Path.cwd(); expanded/resolved root is opened once. Python resolution checks provide diagnostics, while actual reads and writes use the persistent capability. No per-user authentication or tenant partition is implemented by this stdio adapter. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-108, jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:373-381, jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1276, src/publication.rs:146-199.
- MCP mutation -> output publication: output paths must pass portable-relative-name validation; target symlinks/directories are refused, and overwrite=false uses atomic hard-link installation. All bytes are staged and verified before any commit; atomicity is per file, and errors can report an already committed subset. Unix staging directories use mode 0700; Windows inherits platform access controls. Evidence: src/publication.rs:83-143, src/publication.rs:168-199, src/publication.rs:228-245, src/publication.rs:431-512, src/publication.rs:535-570.
- Local CLI caller -> ambient filesystem: native publish_paths holds destination-parent handles and stages outputs, but deliberately grants ambient local-path authority. Python CLI _write uses Path.write_bytes; Node CLI write uses fs.writeFileSync. Their preflight existence checks are distinct from native publication enforcement and must be reviewed independently. Evidence: src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273, src/publication.rs:203-225, jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-103, jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:126-133, jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:76-79, jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:106-111.
- Markdown image names -> native CLI resource loader: the trusted root is explicit --resource-path or the Markdown file's parent, falling back to '.'. URL schemes are refused, percent escapes are decoded before policy checks, canonicalized targets must remain under the root, and actual reads use OutputRoot. The general Markdown library instead accepts a caller-provided image callback, absent by default. Evidence: src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2878, src/bin/jubarte.rs:2936-2943, src/markdown/mod.rs:196-198, src/markdown/mod.rs:216-226.
- Document font requests -> ambient native font discovery/index: document family names select parsed host fonts, including system, Word and cloud-cache locations and configured user fonts. Cloud-family names must be one normal path component. The persistent index records source paths and timestamps, reloads matching files and saves through a process-specific temporary filename followed by rename. These operations do not use the MCP OutputRoot. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236, src/convert/font.rs:2263-2315, src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652, src/convert/font.rs:3154-3172, src/convert/font.rs:3182-3229.
- Operator -> updater -> installed executable: explicit self-update resolves releases from jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, selects one platform archive and inner jubarte binary, requires checksum verification and registers an extracted-regular-file verification hook. Noninteractive installation needs --yes; --check only resolves availability; explicitly requested versions can downgrade. Signature verification is implemented but inactive with the current empty key list. Extraction, HTTP behavior and executable replacement are delegated to self_update and were not independently inspected here. Evidence: src/update.rs:25-48, src/update.rs:88-124, src/update.rs:144-161, src/update.rs:186-258, src/update.rs:307-321.
- Maintainer/CI -> release consumers: tag-driven GitHub builds have contents:read, while the release job alone has contents:write. Optional ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY reaches the signing step and temporary $RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.XXXXXX/keyN files; checksums are generated after signing. A separate local release script can publish crates.io, two npm packages, PyPI and a GitHub fallback release using operator credentials. Its typed version confirmation can be bypassed with the explicit --yes option, and security-check.sh runs even with --skip-gates. Evidence: .github/workflows/release.yml:15-25, .github/workflows/release.yml:51-89, .github/workflows/release.yml:230-321, scripts/release.sh:374-380, scripts/release.sh:655-660, scripts/release.sh:875-899, scripts/release.sh:919-959, scripts/release.sh:976-1012, scripts/release.sh:1039-1069.
- PR content -> privileged review/remediation automation: one pinned reviewer receives JULES_API_KEY and a GitHub token with contents:read, pull-requests:write and checks:write, gated to same-repository non-Dependabot PRs, without local checkout. A different pinned companion-remediation action receives JULES_API_KEY and starts only for trusted arthrod author/dispatcher conditions; its requested mode is AUTO_CREATE_PR with require_plan_approval:false. Companion workflow document gates and prompts do not establish what the external action or Jules service enforces. Evidence: .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:17-50, .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:46-54, .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:65-102, .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:259-324.
- Untrusted DOCX -> admission -> OPC/XML: admission checks pre-index budgets, duplicate decoded/case-folded part names, ZIP structural defects, safe names, bounded inflation and XML policy; it does not extract entries to disk (src/admission.rs:439-490,522-575,736-760,788-803). The shared checked ZIP opener applies structural defects independently of higher-level admission (src/opc/archive.rs:34-51).
- Raw XML library caller -> DOM: try_parse_xdocument_with_limits validates before constructing a DOM; infallible parse_xdocument deliberately skips policy and requires caller validation. Attribute defaults are 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte names, 64 MiB values and 128 MiB complete start tags (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:48-57,90-135). Low-level APIs must not inherit guarantees of higher-level admitted entrypoints merely by association.
- Caller edit plan -> source snapshot -> candidate: v1 source_sha256 is optional, verified when supplied; every operation resolves against the untouched source, and preview/application share preparation. The native CLI additionally rereads and hashes the named source before first publication and checks output aliases/collisions (src/edit.rs:5-24,97-106,2073-2094; src/bin/jubarte.rs:1642-1655,1764-1823). A hash is a freshness/integrity guard, not user authentication.
- Native CLI caller -> ambient output paths: publication opens and holds each parent, stages complete bytes in parent/.jubarte-<UUID>.partial/bytes, and commits per file. Existing files require overwrite/force. Set publication is not atomic; partial commits are reported. Ambient publication replaces a destination symlink itself instead of following its referent (src/publication.rs:168-174,203-225,421-514; src/bin/jubarte.rs:1764-1823).
- MCP stdio client -> document files: --root defaults to cwd, resolves once, and becomes native OutputRoot authority. Path canonicalization is an early diagnostic; actual reads/writes use root-relative cap-std operations. Output paths reject nonportable names and output symlinks, and overwrite defaults false. All tools share this root; read/write annotations are hints rather than separate caller authorization (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:146-199,228-245,431-462).
- Native Markdown -> local image loader: explicit --resource-path overrides the Markdown input's parent, defaulting to '.', and grants that directory's read capability. Percent-decoded URL schemes and resolved paths outside the canonical root are refused; reads use the opened root capability (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2877,2936-2943). npm's adapter rejects resource_path (jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:724-727).
- Native render -> font filesystem/cache: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides user font locations; otherwise macOS uses HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts, Linux uses XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts or HOME/.local/share/jubarte/fonts, Windows uses APPDATA/jubarte/fonts. System/Word font directories and cached face paths are separate ambient reads. Font index precedence is JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX, with empty/off disabling persistence, otherwise parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv. Saves use the index path with extension tsv.<pid>.tmp followed by rename. These operations are outside OutputRoot enforcement (src/convert/font.rs:2263-2275,2281-2315,2637-2652,2855-2862,3095-3107,3156-3165,3183-3189,3213-3229).
- Browser host -> WASM executable: default initialization fetches jubarte_wasm_bg.wasm beside the JavaScript module; the caller may supply another module/URL. This is a code-loading capability, distinct from document relationships. Browser/WASM byte APIs do not expose native publication; Python/npm host wrappers separately perform filesystem I/O (jubarte-wasm/npm/web/jubarte_wasm.js:2575-2594; src/lib.rs:163-165; Cargo.toml:150-153).
- Explicit self-update caller -> GitHub release -> installed executable: fixed repository jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, platform archive and SHA256SUMS.txt; noninteractive installation requires --yes, while --check only looks up. Selected versions may downgrade. The binary inside the archive is jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe]; an extracted-binary lstat hook requires a regular file. Current RELEASE_KEYS is empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None, so signatures are not enforced. Unix uses tar.gz and Windows zip; extraction/replacement are dependency-owned (src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,144-159,198-257,307-320; Cargo.toml:137-148).
- Maintainer publication -> public registries/release consumers: v* tag workflow builds binaries and wheels with read-only job permissions, then a distinct contents:write job publishes GitHub assets. ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY is exposed only to the signing step, decoded under RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.XXXXXX/keyN and removed by an EXIT trap. Missing secrets are accepted only while no public keys are committed. Local release.sh separately publishes crates.io jubarte-redlines, npm jubarte-wasm and jubarte-redlines, and PyPI jubarte-redlines using operator credentials after a typed version confirmation unless skipped (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-89,230-309,356-369; scripts/release.sh:875-889,919-959,1063-1069).
- Developer tooling has separate ambient capabilities: jubarte-rust-inproc accepts COMPARE paths over stdin and directly reads/writes them; it is a benchmark worker, not a root-confined service. The evaluation harness launches configured agent argv in a run directory and new process session with captured stdout/stderr and a timeout; it does not establish an OS sandbox through these subprocess arguments (jubarte-rust-inproc/src/main.rs:5-20,29-68,91-99; eval/cli-v2-pilot/harness/exec.py:191-222,240-249).
- Validated audit qualifications: extension-selected admission misses relationship-selected auxiliary XML; ignored DOCTYPE events differ from permissive DOM declaration boundaries; Markdown drawing dependency graphs have no cycle check. These are retained as separate findings with source evidence, not safe-control assumptions (src/admission.rs:533-569,788-821; src/convert/mod.rs:3480-3497,20768-20795; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:539-546,660-663; src/markdown/from_docx/drawing.rs:1397-1408,1964-1974).
- Concrete MCP artifact consumers use <root>/<out> or <root>/<out_dir>/{clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff,report.json,redline.pdf,redline-page-NN.png}; rendering writes page-NN.png and render.pdf. Return recipients are the connected client and root filesystem readers, with OutputRoot publication and overwrite=false (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:195-255). Ordinary Python path.write_bytes and Node fs.writeFileSync remain ambient host-selected output operations (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:126-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:106-111).
- Concrete ambient font resources: macOS scans /System/Library/Fonts[/Supplemental], /Library/Fonts[/Microsoft] and /Applications/Microsoft Word.app/Contents/Resources/DFonts; cloud families resolve beneath HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts and require one normal path component. Windows system fonts derive from WINDIR else SystemRoot else C:\Windows, plus LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts (src/convert/font.rs:2228-2236,2281-2315,2855-2862). Font index overrides may be relative to process cwd; absent overrides derive parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv, giving HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv on macOS, XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/font-index.tsv or HOME/.local/share/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Linux, APPDATA/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Windows. Absent HOME/APPDATA can disable default paths; index empty/off disables persistence (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3098-3107).
- Developer evaluation artifacts resolve to run_dir/stdout.txt, run_dir/stderr.txt and run_dir/task.output_path, and the configured executable inherits process authority. start_new_session and timeout/process-group termination are lifecycle controls, not filesystem/network authorization (eval/cli-v2-pilot/harness/exec.py:191-222,240-249).
- Untrusted document producer -> engine process: complete DOCX bytes and package metadata cross into ZIP/XML/image/font parsers. Admission performs preindex budgeting and ZIP defect refusal before later processing; the production OPC opener repeats structural consistency checks. Debug triage deliberately opens unchecked and reports defects instead of applying the normal opener's refusal (src/admission.rs:439-470; src/opc/archive.rs:15-51; src/debug.rs:213-215).
- Untrusted raw XML -> public XML APIs: try_parse_xdocument defaults to InputLimits::default and its explicit-limit variant validates before DOM allocation. Infallible parse_xdocument deliberately provides no input-policy enforcement; callers must validate first (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:90-135). This is an explicit API contract, not an established bypass.
- MCP client -> root-granted filesystem operations: stdio server accepts --root, default cwd, resolves it once, constructs native _OutputRoot, reads through the held capability and publishes capability-relative names. Path.resolve supplies early diagnostics rather than final authorization. Tool annotations are descriptive hints; the filesystem capability owns confinement (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- MCP client -> modification authority inside the granted root: edit writes out_dir/clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff, report.json and optional rendered files; render writes page-NN.png and optional render.pdf. Existing targets require overwrite=true. No distinct per-user or per-tool access policy is shown within that root (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:153-218,220-255; src/publication.rs:448-467,558-570).
- Local CLI operator -> ambient filesystem: native CLI reads user-specified paths and grants ambient publication authority. Python and Node adapters separately use host read/write APIs; they must not inherit the stronger native publisher's atomicity/confinement claims merely because they expose the same command names (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2740-2769; src/publication.rs:203-225; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:61-89,101-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-112).
- Markdown contents -> native CLI resource reads: --resource-path overrides the Markdown file's parent directory, falling back to cwd for a bare filename. Targets are percent-decoded, URL schemes refused, canonicalized beneath the root, then read through a held directory capability (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2879,2936-2943). This is a separate, bounded local-read capability, not external relationship fetching.
- Document font names -> native rendering environment: renderer consults installed/user/cloud fonts and a persistent index using process environment and ambient filesystem access. Cloud font family path construction requires one normal component; index validity uses header/version and source stamps. This host-font activity is separate from MCP document path confinement (src/convert/mod.rs:857-878; src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2258-2276,2637-2652,3100-3107,3154-3170,3314-3358).
- Browser/Node host -> WASM module load and byte APIs: package exports select browser or Node loaders; browser default initialization fetches a sibling jubarte_wasm_bg.wasm, while Node reads its installed module file. These are executable-module acquisition paths, distinct from document relationship processing (jubarte-wasm/npm/package.json:34-75; jubarte-wasm/npm/web/jubarte_wasm.js:2571-2592; jubarte-wasm/npm/node/jubarte_wasm.js:2553-2555).
- Operator -> updater -> GitHub release executable: explicit self-update performs preflight, release selection, optional confirmation and checksum verification. The archive binary path is jubarte-{version}-{platform}/jubarte plus platform executable suffix; extracted binary must be a regular file before installation (src/update.rs:102-125,198-257,307-321).
- Maintainer/tag authority -> distribution channels: v* tag push triggers read-only build jobs and a contents:write release job. Signing secret is supplied only to the signing step, decoded under RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.XXXXXX and removed by trap; publish step receives GITHUB_TOKEN. Local release script has a typed version confirmation before pushing and registry publication, and verifies the tag's engine artifact stamp (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,230-235,261-307,356-369; scripts/release.sh:870-919,934-958).
- Lower-trust PR contributor -> shared self-hosted CI: ci/adoption/docs/md-roundtrip subscribe to pull_request and execute the checkout on self-hosted, which can reach a shared tokenless private mbx cache and reused checkout/disk (.github/workflows/ci.yml:13-17,34-48; .github/workflows/adoption.yml:61-100; .github/workflows/docs.yml:79-99; .github/workflows/md-roundtrip.yml:28-39; .github/actions/mbx/action.yml:8-32). External PR-run approval and disposable runner provisioning remain unknown.
- Untrusted document producer -> host process -> package admission and parsers. The host supplies bytes; admission implements archive size/count/inflation and structure controls before consumers parse input, including the shared ZIP structural pass. No disk extraction or external relationship fetching is part of admission. Public parse_xdocument deliberately does not enforce input policy; checked variants validate before DOM construction. Enforcement defects found in this audit remain separate from these intended controls. Evidence: src/admission.rs:13-28,397-407,439-470; src/opc/archive.rs:15-51; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:85-135.
- Document/edit-plan producer -> edit transaction -> transformed package bytes. Plans have schema checks, optional source_sha256 verification, exact-anchor failure modes and signed/macro-bearing source refusal; apply_plan returns prepared results rather than publishing files. Snapshot hashing authenticates neither author nor caller. Evidence: src/edit.rs:1740-1761,2073-2105; src/inspect.rs:273-277.
- MCP client/model -> stdio server -> explicit root-relative input reads. Startup resolves --root or current working directory, opens one native OutputRoot and passes lexical relative names to read. Python resolution is an early diagnostic; actual confinement belongs to held cap-std directory capability. Tools do not establish independent users/tenants. Gemini extension starts uvx --from jubarte-redlines[mcp] jubarte-mcp --root ${workspacePath}, giving the same capability rooted at the host-selected workspace. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-89,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1262; src/publication.rs:146-165; gemini-extension.json:5-8.
- MCP client/model -> brokered publication under the same held root. docx_edit derives out_dir/clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff and report.json, optionally redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png; docx_render derives out_dir/page-NN.png and render.pdf; compare/accept/reject use caller-selected output names. OutputRoot validates portable relative names, capability directories and output symlinks, stages/verifies files, and atomically installs each without clobber unless overwrite=true. Atomicity is per file; errors report committed files. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:101-112,185-217,229-277,285-298; src/publication.rs:83-143,168-199,228-273,431-482,535-570.
- MCP registration/annotations -> external host approval policy. read_only_hint and destructive_hint are hints, not server-side caller authentication or approval enforcement. Host chooses approval policy; admitted calls can request overwrite=true and share a root. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,152-160,220-228; docs/adoption/mcp.md:39-41,168-169.
- Native CLI operator -> ambient local output paths. publish_paths holds selected parent directory handles and stages verified bytes under sibling .jubarte-<UUID>.partial/bytes with private Unix permissions; typed edit publication rechecks input hash and output aliases/collisions. Partial commits may remain after a later error. This grants ambient access rather than MCP root confinement. Evidence: src/publication.rs:203-225,431-505,535-570; src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273,1642-1656,1764-1812.
- Python/Node CLI operator -> ambient filesystem. Python _write uses Path.write_bytes and Node write uses fs.writeFileSync after adapter path/force checks; these do not inherit native publication merely from shared engine grammar. Python Document.read uses ambient read_bytes; in-memory operations return bytes. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/document.py:117-145; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:116-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-111.
- Document-selected font family -> native renderer -> ambient font stores using std::fs, outside MCP OutputRoot. Own-font directory precedence: nonempty JUBARTE_FONT_DIR, otherwise macOS HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts; other Unix XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts or HOME/.local/share/jubarte/fonts (HOME required); Windows APPDATA/jubarte/fonts. System/Word discovery separately uses /System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental, /Library/Fonts, /Applications/Microsoft Word.app/Contents/Resources/DFonts, /Library/Fonts/Microsoft, /System/Library/Fonts and HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts/<family>. Cloud family is one normal path component. Windows discovery uses WINDIR, else SystemRoot, else C:\Windows joined with Fonts, plus LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts. These resources feed font parser/shaper and rendered output, controlled by ambient OS permissions. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2230-2235,2258-2315,2324-2376,2460-2467,2633-2652,2814-2862.
- Native renderer -> persistent ambient font-index state. JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX empty/off disables; another value is complete path; absent value derives parent(user_font_dir())/font-index.tsv. Effective defaults: HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv on macOS, XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/font-index.tsv or HOME/.local/share/jubarte/font-index.tsv on other Unix, APPDATA/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Windows. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides instead derive its parent/font-index.tsv. Reads reuse stamped source paths; writes create parent, write sibling font-index.tsv.<pid>.tmp then rename. No MCP-root control applies; OS permissions, index version/header, file/directory stamps are effective controls. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3095-3112,3154-3188,3213-3229.
- JS caller -> WASM linear memory -> shared engine. WASM exports take byte arrays/JSON and return bytes/results, omit self-update, and cfg-exclude native publication. Node CLI separately supplies filesystem authority. WASM compare inherits roomier 2 GiB inflated comparison budget and documentation tells browser hosts to lower it. Evidence: jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-32; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:63-97; src/lib.rs:163-165; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-55,106-111.
- Operator -> explicit native self-update -> GitHub release/archive/SHA256SUMS.txt -> executable swap. Fixed jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines and OS/arch target select archive and member jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte (Windows jubarte.exe). macOS/Linux use tar.gz, Windows ZIP; extraction/swap delegated to self_update. Noninteractive requires --check or --yes before network lookup; interactive installation confirms. verify_binary rejects nonregular extracted binary. RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None mean checksum-only verification, matching staged-signing docs. Evidence: src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,186-210,212-257; Cargo.toml:137-148; docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:22-45,57-87.
