- Maintainer/tag authority -> GitHub Actions builds -> release publication. v* tag triggers tag/version check and per-platform locked read-only builds; release job gets contents:write and same-run artifacts. Optional signing alone receives ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY, decodes to RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN, signs dist archives, verifies committed keys/*.pub and deletes keys on exit. Checksums follow signing. GITHUB_TOKEN maps to GH_TOKEN for gh release create at GITHUB_REF_NAME in github.repository, notes.md and dist/*. Local release.sh additionally publishes registries and runs security-check.sh even when ordinary gates skipped. Evidence: .github/workflows/release.yml:15-25,40-49,51-89,235-308,317-322,356-369; scripts/release.sh:72-84,105,375-380,654-678.
- Developer benchmark worker -> ambient paths over stdin COMPARE basePath nextPath outPath -> std::fs reads/writes. It is a same-user speed harness, not a hosted network service; independently exposed deployment would require separate controls. Evidence: jubarte-rust-inproc/src/main.rs:5-20,29-69,91-99.
- Untrusted DOCX bytes enter Rust processing through byte APIs and bindings. Admission limits compressed size, entry count, per-part/aggregate inflation, XML nesting and tag policy; no archive extraction, macro execution or external-reference resolution is intended (src/admission.rs:13-28,63-100; src/document_comparer.rs:6530-6567; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:19-36).
- Archive metadata crosses into package interpretation. Normal open_archive invokes zip_defects and refuses inconsistent headers, overlapping/extending entries and unsupported entry types; the deliberately unchecked debug opener is a distinct maintainer inspection path (src/opc/archive.rs:15-51).
- Raw XML callers may choose permissive parse_xdocument, which explicitly does not enforce input policy. Checked parsing or prior admission is the caller's responsibility; parsing must not be modeled as uniformly guarded (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:19-36,84-98; SECURITY.md:54-65).
- An edit-plan producer controls operations, replacement content and optional snapshot guard. Source hash validation is conditional on source_sha256 being present; semantic edits return memory results before host publication (src/edit.rs:1735-1761,2081-2089).
- An MCP client controls tool names, document paths, output paths, plans, rendering options and overwrite flags through a stdio connection. Every registered caller shares the configured directory authority; annotation hints are metadata rather than per-caller authorization. Root is --root or current directory, opened once through _OutputRoot. Python resolution is an early diagnostic; actual reads and writes use the capability (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:146-200).
- MCP document I/O and local CLI publication have independently enforced authority. OutputRoot validates portable relative paths and rejects output symlinks; publish_paths grants ambient local access. Publication stages every file first but commits per file, so a commit/durability failure can expose a reported partial set. Windows parent-directory sync is explicitly a no-op (src/publication.rs:83-143,168-225,228-273,387-405,431-480).
- Python and Node CLI adapters own ambient file access and do not inherit MCP confinement or native publication controls. Their general write helpers directly write destinations after adapter checks (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-111).
- Native rendering crosses into ambient font files and persistent font-index state, independently of document publication authority. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides the platform font directory; JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides or disables the index, otherwise the index is in the font directory's parent. Document family names used in cloud-font paths are restricted to one normal path component (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2633-2652,3098-3107,3154-3169,3213-3229).
- Explicit self-update grants downloaded release code authority to replace the running executable. Repository/target/archive member and SHA256SUMS.txt are configured in code, a prompt or --yes gates installation, and a regular-file hook validates the extracted binary. Current compiled keys are empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None, so signature enforcement is currently inactive (src/update.rs:25-48,91-109,120-125,198-210,234-257).
- Tag pushes trigger build jobs with contents:read, then a contents:write release job publishes dist/* using GITHUB_TOKEN. Optional ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY reaches the signing step and temporary decoded key files; signatures precede checksums. The local release script separately uses operator registry identities and verifies artifact provenance against the tag (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-89,230-239,252-321,356-369; scripts/release.sh:872-899,919-959,1069).
- Markdown input contains active reconstruction metadata: drawing payload JSON indexes form an attacker-controlled dependency graph; table comments encode expansion counts. Optional reference DOCX templates cross a separate package/XML boundary before styles/section extraction (src/markdown/write.rs:59-108,1096-1123,1256-1283,4183-4201; src/markdown/from_docx/drawing.rs:1395-1409,1881-1904,1954-1983; src/markdown/package.rs:101-126,542-557). Legacy DOC story field-control characters cross a distinct parser-work boundary (src/legacy_doc.rs:180-215,1591-1620).
- Document supplier -> in-process package admission -> XML/DOM/media consumers. Admission checks declared/streamed budgets, duplicate names and ZIP defects; comparison admits both active-setting inputs (src/admission.rs:439-470; src/document_comparer.rs:6530-6567). Ordinary budgets and comparison budgets differ (src/admission.rs:63-99).
- Raw XML caller -> checked try_parse_xdocument_with_limits versus intentionally permissive parse_xdocument. Checked parsing validates before construction; permissive parsing requires caller gating (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:84-135).
- MCP --root defaults cwd -> expanduser/resolve once -> native _OutputRoot -> retained cap-std directory. Consumer document read effective path <root>/<client-relative input>; output edit effective paths <root>/<out_dir>/{clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff,report.json,optional redline.pdf/redline-page-NN.png}; render outputs <root>/<out_dir>/{page-NN.png,optional render.pdf}. Python containment is diagnostic; native capability-relative reads/publish enforce actual I/O, overwrite is explicit (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,170-255,373-381; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- All connected stdio MCP callers share exposed tool authority. Read/write annotations are descriptive, not independent authentication or tool-level authorization (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,126-159,373-381).
- Native CLI explicit destinations -> publish_paths(root=None) -> ambient OS filesystem permissions, staged verified replacement. Node CLI explicit paths -> fs.readFileSync/writeFileSync with existsSync precheck; it does not inherit native publication guarantees (src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; src/publication.rs:203-225; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-111,137-145).
- Native document font requests -> ambient lookup: nonempty JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides user directory; defaults macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts, Linux ${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/jubarte/fonts, Windows $APPDATA/jubarte/fonts; installed/Word/cloud directories also searched. Recipients are native font parsers and renderer; OS permissions apply independently of MCP root (src/convert/font.rs:2258-2275,2281-2315,2637-2652).
- Native persistent index consumer: JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX explicit path wins; empty/off disables; absent -> parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv. Effective defaults macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Linux ${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Windows $APPDATA/jubarte/font-index.tsv; JUBARTE_FONT_DIR changes default to its parent's index. Cache is consumed by subsequent renderer processes under OS permissions; save writes sibling path.with_extension(tsv.<pid>.tmp), then renames (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3100-3112,3154-3188,3192-3229).
- Explicit updater -> fixed jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines release and supported platform -> selected tag -> SHA256SUMS.txt -> archive child jubarte-<version>-<platform>-<arch>/jubarte[.exe] -> delegated executable install. Terminal/--yes preflight, check-only mode, checksum and regular-file hook enforce separate controls. RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None mean signatures are not currently required (src/update.rs:25-48,87-108,120-125,186-257,299-310).
- Tag-driven maintainer release -> five native targets -> dist/jubarte-*.tar.gz or .zip. secrets.ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY -> signing-step environment -> base64 decode -> $RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.XXXXXX/keyN -> zipsign; 64-byte key check, cleanup trap and committed-key verification; optional secret only when no public keys committed. SHA256SUMS.txt generated after signing. secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN -> GH_TOKEN -> gh release create github.repository/GITHUB_REF_NAME with notes.md and dist/* (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,58-144,261-322,356-369).
- scripts/release.sh registry publication uses maintainer cargo/npm/uv credentials independently of document processing (scripts/release.sh:370-380,878-919,924-959,1069-1070).
- Untrusted bytes enter public APIs and adapters. Admission controls defaults64MiB compressed,10000 entries,64MiB part,256MiB aggregate,depth256; compare permits512MiB compressed/part and2GiB aggregate. Input controls do not isolate the process. src/admission.rs:13-28,63-99.
- ZIP metadata passes regular open_archive/zip_defects refusal; unchecked opener is distinct and used for typed admission checks and maintainer debugging. src/opc/archive.rs:15-51.
- MCP operator sets --root (default cwd), expanded/resolved once; Python lexical-path diagnostics precede native persistent _OutputRoot reads/publication. Read/write tools share stdio without distinct caller roles; annotations are hints. jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,126-159,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:153-199.
- MCP root-relative writes reject nonportable names/output symlinks, stage every file, require overwrite=true for existing outputs, then atomically commit per file; later failure can leave prior commits. src/publication.rs:168-199,431-463,558-569.
- Native and npm CLIs intentionally use ambient host authority; npm node:fs reads/writes caller paths and checks existing outputs. src/publication.rs:203-205; src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-110.
- Native rendering consumes ambient installed font files and independently derived font index via std::fs, outside explicit MCP root I/O. Browser WASM uses byte rendering; native system/cloud font overrides are no-ops. src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,2855-2876,3095-3106,3213-3229; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:231-235,262-273.
- Explicit self-update selects fixed GitHub owner/repo and platform release, delegates checksum/download/extraction/swap to self_update; RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None disable signature requirement. src/update.rs:25-48,120-125,198-221,234-257.
- Privileged release tag workflow builds with contents:read, publishes with contents:write; signing secret only enters its step, decodes under RUNNER_TEMP and EXIT trap deletes keys. Local release script separately publishes registries. .github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-55,230-235,261-309,356-369; scripts/release.sh:919-955,1063-1069.
- Document supplier to engine: attacker-controlled DOCX bytes cross archive admission, OPC loading, XML validation, comparison/editing, and rendering. Default admission allows 64 MiB compressed, 10,000 entries, 64 MiB per part, 256 MiB inflated, and XML depth 256; comparison permits 512 MiB compressed/per part and 2 GiB inflated (src/admission.rs:63-100). Structural ZIP checks compare central/local records and reject recorded defects; actual decompression is bounded independently of declared sizes (src/admission.rs:439-470; src/opc/archive.rs:34-51; src/opc/mod.rs:275-324).
- Caller-controlled input limits to binding configuration: Python and WASM parse overrides through InputLimitOverrides and apply them over comparison settings. Unknown keys are rejected; applications own any decision to raise or lower these limits (src/admission.rs:103-138; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:65-79; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:63-85).
- Raw XML library caller to DOM: parse_xdocument is intentionally permissive and does not enforce input policy; try_parse_xdocument_with_limits validates before constructing a DOM (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:90-135). Package admission must not be inferred for arbitrary direct XML API use.
- MCP client/model to filesystem tools: --root defaults to startup cwd and is resolved once; path arguments receive Python containment diagnostics, but actual document reads/publication use native OutputRoot (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,373-381). OutputRoot is the enforcement owner, not MCP annotations. Read-only/destructive hints are tool metadata, and client approval remains host-owned (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55; docs/adoption/mcp.md:39-41).
- MCP publication: edits derive clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff and report.json below caller-selected out_dir; optional render products share that directory. Native publication validates names and stages every output before committing, rejects unsafe paths, and uses overwrite=false unless granted. Commit atomicity is per file, not across the set; errors identify already committed files (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:152-217; src/publication.rs:83-143,168-199).
- Native CLI to filesystem: document input paths are read with std::fs::read; authorized output paths are handed to ambient publication. There is no MCP-style root capability for the entire CLI (src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273,2740-2756; src/publication.rs:203-225). Python Document.read and Node CLI similarly read explicitly supplied paths; Node writes use fs.writeFileSync (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/document.py:142-145; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-73,106-111).
- Markdown-controlled image references to local resources: the native CLI resolves --resource-path or the Markdown input's resource directory, percent-decodes targets, refuses URL schemes and escapes, then reads through a held OutputRoot capability (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2870,3365-3375). Library DocxOptions instead accepts a caller-provided ImageLoader callback and defaults to no loader; callback authority belongs to the embedding application (src/markdown/mod.rs:159-170).
- Native render request to ambient font state: requested families select installed/supplemental font searches and FONT_INDEX lookups. These reads and index writes use ambient fs operations, independent of MCP document-root confinement (src/convert/font.rs:2258-2315,2637-2652,3154-3188,3213-3227). Windows system font locations derive from WINDIR, then SystemRoot, then C:\Windows, plus LOCALAPPDATA\Microsoft\Windows\Fonts (src/convert/font.rs:2855-2862).
- Edit requester to document mutation: optional source_sha256 binds a schema-1 plan to the exact source snapshot and mismatches produce STALE_SOURCE. This is a consistency guard, not authentication or approval (src/edit.rs:2074-2089; SECURITY.md:76-85).
- Maintainer debug mode to malformed packages: debug intentionally uses open_archive_unchecked so rejected archives can be inspected, unlike ordinary package loading; this materially different parser path must retain its own resource assessment (src/debug.rs:213; src/opc/archive.rs:15-20,34-51).
- Operator to self-update: only the explicit self-update command invokes update::run. It selects jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines releases, platform asset and archive binary path, SHA256SUMS.txt, optional signature verification, and an extracted-regular-file hook before delegated replacement (src/bin/jubarte.rs:4062-4073; src/update.rs:25-48,186-210,234-257,299-321).
- Maintainer/tag authority to distribution: v* tags trigger read-only build jobs and a separate contents:write release job. Optional signing receives ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY only in its step, decodes it beneath RUNNER_TEMP, and removes temporary keys on exit; GitHub publication receives GITHUB_TOKEN separately (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,230-307,356-369). Local release.sh independently confirms the target version unless --yes, pushes release state, and publishes using operator registry identities (scripts/release.sh:875-899).
- Effective font resources by deployment: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides supplemental fonts. Otherwise macOS uses $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts; Linux requires HOME and uses nonempty XDG_DATA_HOME or $HOME/.local/share, then jubarte/fonts; Windows uses %APPDATA%\jubarte\fonts. All reads retain process OS authority, including during MCP rendering (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652).
- Effective font index: nonempty JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX selects the exact caller-configured path; empty or off disables it. Otherwise the index is parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv: macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Linux ${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Windows %APPDATA%\jubarte\font-index.tsv. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR therefore changes the default index to its parent, not its child. A temporary sibling uses extension tsv.<pid>.tmp before rename; readers/writers are native renderer processes and index-path principals, protected by ambient OS permissions and freshness checks (src/convert/font.rs:3098-3107,3154-3188,3213-3227).
- Effective MCP products: operator grants canonical startup --root (default cwd); client-selected out_dir yields clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff and report.json, optionally redline.pdf and redline-page-NN.png under that capability. The client receives designated text/reports and paths; document-output writes use overwrite=false unless explicitly selected. Publication stages all bytes, then commits per file and reports partial commits (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,152-217; src/publication.rs:83-143,168-199).
- Effective release signing: ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY is a step-scoped secret reference, decoded to $RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.XXXXXX/keyN and consumed by zipsign for dist/jubarte archives; cleanup trap removes the private files. Missing secret is tolerated only before keys/*.pub exists. GitHub publication separately consumes GITHUB_TOKEN as GH_TOKEN in the contents:write release job, targeting github.repository/GITHUB_REF_NAME. Local registry publication uses operator cargo/npm credentials and UV_PUBLISH_TOKEN, after version confirmation unless --yes (.github/workflows/release.yml:230-307,356-369; scripts/release.sh:370-380,875-899).
- Untrusted document producer -> in-process parser/comparer/renderer. Admission checks package budgets; shared production archive open checks structural defects. Debug deliberately uses unchecked archive open for triage. Engine controls differ from host CPU/memory obligations (src/admission.rs:13-28,63-99; src/opc/archive.rs:15-51; src/convert/mod.rs:769-778).
- Edit-plan producer -> mutation: V2 validates plan, required hash, scope/context and maps operations; legacy source binding is conditional on source_sha256 being present (src/edit/v2_apply.rs:84-111; src/edit.rs:2073-2094).
- MCP client -> operator-granted root. --root or cwd resolves and opens native OutputRoot once. Inputs/outputs pass relative names through held cap_std handles; Python containment is diagnostic. ToolAnnotations are hints, not separate caller authorization/approval (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-108,152-159,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- MCP/native rendering -> ambient fonts/cache: docx_render and optional edit rendering load font resources outside explicit document/output capability. Index path is JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX (empty/off disables), else parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv; user_font_dir is JUBARTE_FONT_DIR when nonempty, else macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts, Windows $APPDATA/jubarte/fonts, Linux $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts or $HOME/.local/share/jubarte/fonts. Temporary sibling replaces extension with tsv.<pid>.tmp. Readers are later native processes/local filesystem users; control is host permissions and index version/stamps, not MCP OutputRoot (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-191,234-255; src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3100-3112,3154-3168,3183-3229).
- Native renderer -> system/Word font discovery: macOS /System/Library/Fonts[/Supplemental], /Library/Fonts[/Microsoft], /Applications/Microsoft Word.app/Contents/Resources/DFonts; Windows WINDIR/SystemRoot/default C:\Windows plus Fonts and LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts; macOS Word cloud $HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts/<family> with one-normal-component family constraint. Host environment/permissions own these resources; font bytes reach parser/shaper/outputs (src/convert/font.rs:2228-2235,2281-2315,2855-2862).
- Local CLI operator -> ambient files: Rust opens selected parent once, stages .jubarte-<UUID>.partial with Unix mode 0700, verifies all bytes and commits per file. Python/Node wrappers directly read/write selected paths under OS authority; no MCP confinement is implied (src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; src/publication.rs:100-130,203-218,440-495; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:61-89,101-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-111).
- MCP exports -> root filesystem readers: edit writes fixed clean.docx/redline.docx/patch.diff/report.json plus optional redline.pdf/redline-page-NN.png below out_dir; render writes page-NN.png and optional render.pdf. overwrite defaults false, native OutputRoot validates names/stages all bytes, but each file commits independently. Responses expose paths/report/text/font information, not document bytes as export payloads (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:152-255; src/publication.rs:100-130,175-199).
- Markdown image references -> native local reads. --resource-path wins, else Markdown parent or '.', canonicalized and held as directory capability; percent-decoding precedes URL rejection, containment and capability-relative reads. Image bytes enter generated DOCX/output. Python/Node reject unsupported resource-path rather than inheriting native resource authority (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2835-2876,2936-2943; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:1058-1062; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:724-726).
- Browser/Node caller -> WASM byte API: host supplies/receives bytes or JSON, native publication is excluded on wasm32; Node CLI separately adds filesystem authority (jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:7-28; src/lib.rs:163-165; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-79,106-111).
- GitHub release authority -> installed executable. Explicit self-update selects fixed jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines and platform, latest/requested version, binary member jubarte-<version>-<platform>/jubarte[.exe], SHA256SUMS.txt, confirmation/--yes and regular-file verifier. Current RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None mean checksum-only; signatures are conditional support (src/update.rs:25-48,83-108,186-258,299-310; docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:59-80).
- Maintainer/tag authority -> publication. v* CI checks version/tag match, read-only build jobs, contents:write release job. Step-scoped ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY is decoded to $RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN, cleanup trap, 64-byte length/committed-public-key checks, signatures before checksums. Missing secret fails only if public keys exist. GH_TOKEN references GITHUB_TOKEN for publishing dist/* to github.repository release ( .github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,40-49,230-321,356-369).
- Operator registry authority -> crates.io jubarte-redlines, npm jubarte-wasm then jubarte-redlines, PyPI dist/pypi wheel/sdist sets. Local registry credentials, optional npm OTP, packaged-summary/complete-wheel-set checks apply; --no-wait explicitly consents to local partial platform set (scripts/release.sh:907-959,1042-1067).
- Redaction producer -> distributed output recipient: untouched package parts retain original bytes, while the intended publication guard scans both clean/redline for redacted text. - Untrusted DOCX bytes cross admission/OPC parsing. Controls cover container budgets, duplicate identities, unsafe names, unsupported encryption/compression, header/extent defects, actual inflated streams and XML policy. Archive names are in-memory keys, not host extraction destinations (src/admission.rs:439-575; src/opc/mod.rs:282-379; src/opc/archive.rs:34-51). Validated entry-count and relationship-work findings qualify these controls.
- Admission differs by consumer: inspect/edit Opened uses default limits, comparison uses settings.input_limits, rendering calls compare-budget admit_package. Binding comparison overrides replace fields and reject unknown keys (src/inspect.rs:604-615; src/document_comparer.rs:4926-4927,6530-6567; src/convert/mod.rs:769-778; src/admission.rs:103-165; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:65-79; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:80-97).
- Checked XML validates before DOM creation: DTD/entity/structure and tag/namespace budgets. Public infallible parse_xdocument is explicitly unchecked. - Legacy DOC is separate OLE parsing, not DOCX admission. It reads WordDocument and the FIB-selected table stream and bounds sector-chain traversal by file size (src/legacy_doc.rs:170-195,658-681,765-803).
- Edit instructions grant document mutation, not identity/authentication. Legacy source_sha256 is optional; PlanV2 hash match required; legacy editing refuses signed/macro-bearing packages. Native publication separately rechecks source/alias state (src/edit.rs:2072-2105; src/edit/v2_apply.rs:72-91; src/bin/jubarte.rs:1748-1824).
- Native CLI explicitly selected paths use ambient authority, held parents, .jubarte-<uuid>.partial/bytes staging, readback, rename overwrite or hard-link no-clobber. MCP OutputRoot uses held root authority, portable relative output names and symlink refusal. Sets commit per file; Windows directory sync is a no-op (src/publication.rs:146-225,228-273,390-411,421-569).
- Python/Node CLIs are separate writers: existence checks then Path.write_bytes/fs.writeFileSync; edit bundles directly write clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff, optional renders and report.jsonl in out_dir. They do not inherit native publication by sharing command grammar (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-133,478-517; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:76-109,517-552).
- MCP stdio clients control tool arguments; operator chooses --root or startup cwd, Gemini passes ${workspacePath}. build_server resolves once and opens _OutputRoot; actual document reads/publications are capability-relative, Python resolve is diagnostic. ToolAnnotations are hints, not caller auth; clients may request overwrite=true within root (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-108,152-160,258-277,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; gemini-extension.json:5-9).
- Native Markdown image capability: --resource-path, otherwise input parent or '.', canonical root -> percent-decoded contained path -> OutputRoot read; schemes refused and unavailable images become alt text. Raw Rust defaults no loader; Python/WASM byte conversion supplies none (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2878,2936-2943,3365-3375; src/markdown/mod.rs:159-170,196-198,216-229; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1200-1218; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:1465-1488).
- Native rendering separately reads configured/per-user and fixed system/Word fonts. macOS cloud family must be one normal component; Windows uses WINDIR/SystemRoot/C:\Windows Fonts and LOCALAPPDATA Microsoft/Windows/Fonts. Index reads recorded face paths/stamps and writes a sibling tsv.<pid>.tmp then rename. WASM uses bundled/document fonts (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2258-2315,2633-2652,2831-2862,3095-3107,3154-3189,3213-3229; README.md:901-920).
- Self-update is explicit native authority: fixed jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines release, platform archive jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe], checksum SHA256SUMS.txt, confirmation/--yes, --check lookup only. RELEASE_KEYS=[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None mean checksum-only currently; verify_binary refuses extracted nonregular files (src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,186-259,274-285,299-321). Dependency default installation is current_exe; fonts are not installed (docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:36-45).
- Release CI is v* tag-triggered with read-only builds and contents:write final publication. ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY is a step-scoped reference decoded into $RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.XXXXXX/keyN, length checked, archives signed/verified against committed public keys when present, and keys removed on exit. GH_TOKEN from secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN permits release create. Local release.sh separately consumes Cargo/npm/gh/UV_PUBLISH_TOKEN authority and confirms version/tag ( .github/workflows/release.yml:15-25,230-239,261-309,317-322,356-369; scripts/release.sh:360-389,870-901,919-959,1039-1069).
- Untrusted pull-request checkout code runs on self-hosted CI/Markdown-roundtrip/Adoption/Docs jobs. The local mbx action documents private tokenless http://mbx-cache:7543, namespace jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, reused checkout/cache/disk state. Workflow token restrictions do not isolate OS/network capabilities ( .github/workflows/ci.yml:13-48,72-99,114-138,165-310; .github/workflows/md-roundtrip.yml:13-39; .github/workflows/adoption.yml:19-99; .github/workflows/docs.yml:42-99; .github/actions/mbx/action.yml:8-32).
- Unpublished jubarte-rust-inproc benchmark worker accepts COMPARE input/output paths over stdin and uses ambient fs reads/writes. It is a trusted harness tool, not MCP-root confined; untrusted protocol exposure is not established (jubarte-rust-inproc/Cargo.toml:5-18; jubarte-rust-inproc/src/main.rs:29-99).
- Rendering is a derived-representation boundary beyond archive admission: HTML/MHT expansion, table columns, numbering labels/levels, embedded-font ownership, WMF records and font/PDF/MATH parsing must independently bound work. Validated findings establish gaps; OS resource caps remain caller-owned (src/convert/mod.rs:769-808; src/convert/altchunk.rs:463-491; src/convert/pdf.rs:1434-1451,1531-1544).
- Scrub/redaction outputs cross a confidentiality boundary before sharing. Property scrub must remove requested metadata and residual-secret checks must interpret supported encodings before accepting output; alternate metadata names and UTF16 scanning gaps were validated (src/scrub.rs:357-384,463-475; src/edit.rs:4727-4746).
- Untrusted DOCX supplier -> ZIP/OPC/XML engine -> in-memory parts/DOM. Admission enforces compressed/entry/inflation/XML budgets, duplicate identities and ZIP consistency before use; admitted main parts are checked even when their filename lacks an XML extension. PartFs::open separately defaults to compare budgets rather than InputLimits::default (src/admission.rs:439-470,473-513,516-569; src/opc/mod.rs:488-524). Checked XML APIs enforce policy; infallible parse_xdocument is not that gate (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:91-134).
- Caller-provided Markdown and edit plans -> document construction and edits. Exact anchor resolution and optional SHA-256 source checking protect document intent, but caller-selected author/plan/source are not authenticated identities. Legacy source_sha256 is optional (src/lib.rs:51-64; src/edit.rs:2073-2094). Legacy binary DOC import is a distinct parser path and intentionally omits pictures, macros and many ancillary stories; encryption/obfuscation is refused (src/legacy_doc.rs:13-50).
- Native Markdown image names -> host image files. --resource-path overrides the Markdown parent's directory (or cwd). URL schemes are refused after percent decoding; canonical containment plus a held OutputRoot capability confines the actual read to that resource root. This capability is independent of ambient CLI output authority (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2870,2936-2943).
- Local native CLI operator -> ambient document/output paths. publish_paths grants ambient access to explicitly chosen output parents. Per-file exclusive staging, byte readback and rename/hard-link install enforce replacement/no-clobber semantics; errors preserve committed-path/durability state. This is not a hosted-root sandbox (src/publication.rs:203-225,419-470,480-505,533-570).
- Python/npm CLI operator -> ambient host files through adapter-owned I/O. Python _write directly calls Path.write_bytes and npm write directly calls fs.writeFileSync; their plan recorders use predictable PID partial siblings followed by replace/rename. Native publisher guarantees must not be attributed to these independent implementations (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:126-133,403-414; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:106-111,408-426,539-552).
- MCP stdio client -> shared root-granted read/edit/render tools -> native _OutputRoot. Python resolved-path checks provide diagnostics; actual document reads/writes delegate to Rust held-directory capability. Writes default to overwrite=false; names are validated, output symlinks refused and no-clobber installs use hard links. Tool annotations are hints, not separate read/write authorization. The configured client can call all registered tools; no tenant/session isolation was established (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,126-168,374-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:161-199,448-463,563-569).
- Native renderer -> ambient installed font files and persistent index. Document family names influence font selection; cloud-family directory names must be a single normal path component. Font-index entries contain filesystem paths/stamps and are loaded/read/saved outside the MCP document capability using ambient std::fs. MCP --root therefore constrains adapter document I/O, not every renderer resource (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2258-2315,2637-2652,3095-3106,3155-3169,3183-3229). Browser/WASM host filesystem publication is compiled out (Cargo.toml:150-158; src/lib.rs:163-165).
- Explicit updater invocation -> GitHub release assets -> replacement executable. Preflight refuses noninteractive installation without --yes; --check does not install; normal selection only upgrades, while --version permits explicit downgrades. The updater configures SHA-256 verification and a regular-file extraction hook; trusted signing keys are presently absent. GitHub release asset control is therefore an authenticity prerequisite, not an input-document capability (src/update.rs:120-125,144-160,186-257,307-320).
- Tag-pushing maintainer -> GitHub Actions build/release and registry publication. v* triggers build jobs with contents:read; release job gains contents:write and publishes resulting dist artifacts. Local release script separately requires version confirmation unless --yes, calls mandatory security checks even with --skip-gates, publishes registries and invokes a separately configured app release script. External GitHub settings, registry permissions and external app deployment controls are not established by this repository (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-25,51-89,230-239,261-309,356-369; scripts/release.sh:385-388,655-656,875-895,919-956,1069).
- Parent source validation established that auxiliary relationship-routed XML can evade filename-selected admission and reach unchecked DOMs in converter/comparer/edit consumers (src/admission.rs:533-569; src/convert/font_table.rs:66-78; src/document_comparer.rs:4685-4752,4869-4880; src/edit.rs:2154-2158,5552-5563). Physical ZIP limits also do not budget semantic spans, repeated chunk expansion, logical legacy stories or quadratic metadata/field work. These are retained findings; checked consumers at src/inspect.rs:795-807, src/comments.rs:457-468 and src/edit.rs:1436-1443 remain effective at their call sites.
- Document supplier -> package/parser process: ZIP metadata, entries, XML, relationships, embedded media. Admission checks preindex size/entries, inflation and zip defects. Default 64 MiB compressed/per part, 256 MiB total,10000 entries,depth256; compare/render/WmlDocument 512 MiB compressed/per part,2 GiB total,same depth/entries. src/admission.rs:63-100,397-425,439-470; src/opc/archive.rs:34-51; src/inspect.rs:605-615; src/document_comparer.rs:4926-4927; src/convert/mod.rs:773.
- Raw XML -> DOM: checked APIs enforce InputLimits/tag policy; infallible parse_xdocument is permissive. Defaults 1024 attrs,1024-byte names,64 MiB values,128 MiB start tags. src/xmllinq/parse.rs:19-36,48-57,98-129,910-919; SECURITY.md:43-60.
- Legacy DOC -> compound-file parser: streams/FIB-selected tables -> text/style/list; distinct from DOCX ZIP admission and needs separate resource assessment: src/legacy_doc.rs:170-215,225-236.
- Agent/edit plan -> mutation: schema,optional source hash,author,anchors checked before outputs. Snapshot guard conditional. Plan refusal distinct from publication atomicity: src/edit.rs:1740-1761,2073-2100; src/publication.rs:83-131.
- MCP client -> host files: --root defaults cwd, expanded/resolved once, must directory. Path diagnostics plus actual native _OutputRoot backed cap_std directory. Stdio tools confer root-scoped authority; annotations not per-user authorization. jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1277; src/publication.rs:146-199.
- MCP artifacts: <root>/<out_dir>/{clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff,report.json}, optional redline.pdf,redline-page-NN.png. Capability-relative parents,portable names,output symlink refusal; staging <destination parent>/.jubarte-<UUID>.partial/bytes; byte readback; overwrite=false atomic hard link,overwrite=true rename; per-file partial commits reported. jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-205; src/publication.rs:83-131,168-199,419-505,535-570.
- Native CLI -> ambient selected parents via publish_paths; differs from MCP. Ordinary Python/Node CLI helpers directly Path.write_bytes/fs.writeFileSync after prechecks; common grammar does not prove shared write controls. src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; src/publication.rs:203-210; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:76-79,106-110.
- Markdown -> native image loader: --resource-path else Markdown parent else '.'; percent decode,scheme refusal,canonical containment and actual capability-relative read. Explicit image embedding authority,not external relationship fetching: src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2870,2936-2943.
- Document font request -> renderer-owned local font access: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides; macOS HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts; Linux XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts or HOME/.local/share/jubarte/fonts, requires HOME before XDG lookup; Windows APPDATA/jubarte/fonts. Installed Windows fonts WINDIR else SystemRoot else C:\Windows/Fonts plus LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts. Native catalogue includes /System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental,/Library/Fonts,/Applications/Microsoft Word.app/Contents/Resources/DFonts,/Library/Fonts/Microsoft,/System/Library/Fonts and HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts. Readers renderer/font parser and generated PDF/subsets; controls operator env/host permissions/family matching, not MCP root. src/convert/font.rs:2228-2232,2258-2315,2427-2466,2633-2652,2831-2862.
- WASM host -> byte engine: Uint8Array/JSON same core; native publication excluded wasm32. Browser hosts advised lower compare defaults since 2GiB inflated exceeds practical heap. Node file authority JS host not WASM API: src/lib.rs:163-165; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:7-28,63-95; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-55.
- Self-update -> fixed GitHub release -> jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe] -> installed executable. macos/linux aarch64/x86_64 tar.gz; windows-x86_64 ZIP. Noninteractive requires --yes or --check; --check no replacement; explicit --version allows downgrade. SHA256SUMS.txt and regular-file verify hook; signing currently inactive. Bindings disable updater default features. src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,144-150,198-257,299-315; jubarte-python/Cargo.toml:35-39; jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-20.
- Release tag/operator -> public supply chain: v* -> hosted builds -> contents:write release job; step-scoped ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY base64 -> RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN -> zipsign -> trap cleanup,64-byte key check,committed public-key verification; checksums then gh release create GH_TOKEN=GITHUB_TOKEN. Local script separately Cargo auth -> crates.io jubarte-redlines@$VER; npm auth/NPM_OTP -> jubarte-wasm/jubarte-redlines; UV_PUBLISH_TOKEN -> dist/pypi/* to PyPI with wheel inventory or explicit --no-wait partial fallback. Local artifact recovery downloads completed workflow artifacts to dist/release,creates sdist/checksums and gh release create $TAG without signing step. .github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,40-49,51-89,230-308,317-321,356-369; scripts/release.sh:374-380,876-939,976-1012,1028-1032,1039-1069.
- PR -> internal runner/cache: PR CI/adoption/docs/roundtrip use self-hosted; contents:read and no persisted checkout credentials. Composite mbx remote http://mbx-cache:7543 namespace jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines,cache-links=false,MBX_GC_MAX_TOTAL_SIZE=20GiB. Comments claim private Docker network and protected-push-only publication,not enforced by repository server configuration. Network ACLs,upstream action enforcement and runner lifecycle external. .github/workflows/ci.yml:13-48; .github/workflows/adoption.yml:16-42,48-59; .github/workflows/docs.yml:76-99; .github/actions/mbx/action.yml:8-38.
- PR/dispatch -> review services: Jules Review same-repo non-Dependabot guard,runner var else ubuntu-latest,pinned action receives JULES_API_KEY/GITHUB_TOKEN,contents:read,pull-requests:write,checks:write,enable_approve=false. Conejo resolves/rejects forks,checks exact SHA,no persisted credentials,contents:read,pull-requests:write,issues:write; trusted user arthrod gates session; JULES_API_KEY reaches separate pinned action. External service/install authority absent. .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:19-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:31-54,65-102,262-324.
- Untrusted document producer to engine: complete package bytes enter admission before representative comparison/rendering paths. Default limits are 64 MiB compressed, 10,000 entries, 64 MiB per part, 256 MiB total inflated and XML depth 256; comparison limits are 512 MiB compressed/per part and 2 GiB total, with the same entry/depth caps. Python/WASM comparison options apply explicit overrides to the comparison settings. Admission checks ZIP defects, canonical part identity, encryption/compression, declared and actual inflation and XML policy. Evidence: src/admission.rs:63-99,137-153,439-553; src/document_comparer.rs:4903-4918,6530-6559; src/convert/mod.rs:769-778; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:65-79; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:63-74.
- Raw XML caller to DOM: checked parsing applies XML policy, while public infallible parse_xdocument is a lower-level permissive interface whose caller must gate untrusted XML. The tag defaults are 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte attribute names, 64 MiB raw/decoded values and 128 MiB complete start tags. Evidence: src/xmllinq/parse.rs:44-57,91-98,111-134.
- Agent/client to edit engine: plans select exact anchors against the untouched source; optional legacy source hash rejects stale bytes. This establishes document consistency, not authentication or approval of the requested change. Evidence: src/edit.rs:5-24,2073-2094.
- Native CLI/operator to local filesystem: explicitly selected paths use ambient authority. The Rust publisher holds destination-parent handles, stages and verifies complete bytes, and commits each file separately; failures report already committed files. Existing destinations require overwrite authorization. This is not the MCP directory confinement boundary. Evidence: src/publication.rs:83-130,168-205,211-225,421-480.
- MCP client to document filesystem: --root defaults to process cwd and is expanded/resolved once; build_server opens a native OutputRoot capability. All document loads and outputs route through that held directory. Python resolve/is_relative_to checks provide early diagnostics; actual I/O is capability relative. Outputs reject unsafe relative names, escaping resolution and output symlinks, with overwrite false by default. All connected stdio clients share this root and the registered read/write tools; ToolAnnotations are hints, not per-client authorization. Evidence: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,152-160,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:146-199,228-245,431-463.
- Markdown author to CLI image loader: document image names are percent decoded, URL schemes are refused and resolved targets must remain inside --resource-path or the Markdown file's parent, falling back to cwd. A held OutputRoot performs the actual read. This resource capability is independent of ambient output authority. Evidence: src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2848,2851-2870,2936-2943.
- Document-selected font families to renderer/host: renderer searches installed font locations and its configured per-user directory; a persistent index can directly select stored face paths after file/directory stamp checks. JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides the platform directory. JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides the index path; empty/off disables it. Otherwise the index is a sibling of fonts, at parent(user_font_dir)/font-index.tsv, with staging at the same path after replacing its extension with tsv.<pid>.tmp. These operations use ambient std::fs and do not pass through MCP OutputRoot. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,2814-2862,3098-3107,3155-3168,3182-3229.
- Browser WASM host to byte API: browser/Node wasm-bindgen functions accept and return byte arrays/strings; filesystem publication is separately performed by a host adapter. The Node CLI explicitly has node:fs authority and directly reads/writes caller-selected paths. Do not transfer native Rust publication guarantees to that adapter. Evidence: jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:7-28; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-73,76-79,106-110.
- Release infrastructure to explicit updater: the target is fixed to jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines and the platform-specific archive entry jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe]. Checksums come from SHA256SUMS.txt in the same release. Current RELEASE_KEYS is empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None, so signatures are not enforced. Noninteractive replacement requires --yes; --check performs lookup only; named versions can intentionally downgrade. An extracted-binary hook rejects nonregular files. Evidence: src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,144-160,198-257,274-285,299-320.
- Maintainer/tag authority to release publication: v* tag pushes run a version-match check, read-only build jobs and a distinct contents:write release job. Signing receives secrets.ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY in its signing step, decodes keys into a temporary runner directory, removes that directory on exit and verifies against committed public keys when present. GitHub publication receives secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN and uploads dist/* to github.repository. Evidence: .github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,31-49,51-89,230-245,261-309,317-322,356-369.
- Document supplier to engine: supplied DOCX ZIP/XML, legacy DOC, embedded fonts/images and Markdown are data, not operator configuration. Representative comparison and rendering paths admit packages before processing; admission checks ZIP structural disagreement and package identity (src/document_comparer.rs:4907-4927; src/convert/mod.rs:773-778; src/admission.rs:439-490). Defaults are 64 MiB compressed/per-part, 256 MiB inflated, 10,000 entries and depth 256; comparison permits 512 MiB compressed/per-part and 2 GiB inflated (src/admission.rs:63-99).
- Raw XML caller to DOM: parse_xdocument is deliberately permissive and does not enforce input policy; checked try_parse_xdocument_with_limits validates before DOM construction. Do not collapse these independently exposed APIs into universal automatic admission (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:90-108,119-135). Debug is a deliberate unchecked ZIP-inspection path, distinct from production package refusal (src/opc/archive.rs:16-45; src/debug.rs:213).
- MCP client to document filesystem: --root defaults to server cwd and is expanded/resolved once. _OutputRoot holds cap-std directory authority; actual reads and publication use that authority, rather than trusting Python's earlier resolve checks. All connected callers receive the registered read/write tools; annotations are hints, not user/tenant authorization (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1276; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- MCP write capability: tool-selected out_dir derives clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff, report.json and optional redline.pdf/redline-page-NN.png beneath the root. Native publication rejects unsafe relative names, escaping resolution and output symlinks; overwrite must be requested. Staging is validated before commits, but atomicity is per file and failures can report prior committed files (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-217; src/publication.rs:123-130,168-199,228-250,431-469,558-569).
- CLI operator to ambient filesystem: native publication holds each explicitly selected parent directory, stages bytes in parent/.jubarte-<uuid>.partial/bytes and installs by rename or no-clobber hard link. This grants ambient local access, unlike OutputRoot. Python and npm CLIs instead perform their own ambient direct writes after overwrite checks; shared command grammar does not establish equal filesystem guarantees (src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; src/publication.rs:203-225,480-505,558-569; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-103,126-133,510-517; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:76-79,106-111).
- Markdown image reference to host file: native CLI --resource-path overrides the Markdown source's parent directory, defaulting to '.'. URL-like targets are refused; decoded/canonicalized paths must resolve inside that root and actual reads use its held OutputRoot capability. This is separate from document output publication (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2669-2677,2811-2848,2858-2870,2937-2943).
- Native renderer to ambient font resources: JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides platform user-font locations; system/Word/Office cloud-font directories are also searched. JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides the index path; empty/off disables it; otherwise the default is user_font_dir().parent()/font-index.tsv. Index writes use sibling font-index.tsv.<pid>.tmp followed by rename, outside the MCP document-root capability when the configured font location is outside it (src/convert/font.rs:2228-2235,2281-2315,2637-2652,2855-2862,3100-3106,3213-3229). Effective default font directories are macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/fonts, Linux $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/fonts else $HOME/.local/share/jubarte/fonts, Windows $APPDATA/jubarte/fonts. The default index is one directory above, font-index.tsv. Windows system font roots use WINDIR else SystemRoot else C:\Windows, plus LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts; Office cloud cache is $HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts.
- JS host to WASM: compare accepts borrowed document bytes plus JSON limit overrides and returns bytes/errors. Browser/Node package entrypoints are separate generated loaders; native publication is cfg-excluded on wasm32. Node CLI filesystem authority belongs to its JS adapter, not to the WASM byte API (jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:65-98; jubarte-wasm/npm/package.json:34-75; src/lib.rs:163-165; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-55,106-111).
- Operator to updater to release infrastructure: only explicit self-update enters this flow. Fixed repository jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, target-specific asset and SHA256SUMS.txt feed self_update; --check does not install; confirmation or --yes precedes installation. RELEASE_KEYS is empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None, so this checkout enforces checksum-only updates. A regular-file verification hook rejects extracted symlink/nonregular binaries before swap (src/update.rs:25-48,186-210,223-257,307-321). Windows enables ZIP extraction while other shipped targets use tar.gz (Cargo.toml:144-155; docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:25-36). Archive assets are jubarte-<version>-<target>.tar.gz or .zip, containing jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe]; the independent architecture reviewer inspected self_update 1.3.1 common builder lines1106-1108 and found the install destination defaults to std::env::current_exe(). No actual deployed executable path was queried.
- Maintainer/tag authority to public distribution: v* tags trigger release builds; build jobs have contents:read and release job contents:write. Optional ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY is provided to the signing step, decoded under RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN and removed by trap; signed archives precede checksum generation. GH_TOKEN references GITHUB_TOKEN for gh release create, publishing dist/* to the tagged repository release. The maintainer script separately publishes crates.io jubarte-redlines, npm jubarte-wasm and jubarte-redlines, and PyPI jubarte-redlines using operator-held credentials (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,230-308,317-321,356-369; scripts/release.sh:886-899,919,935-954,1069).
- Legacy DOC logical mapping and field-state boundaries are distinct from ZIP/XML policy: the convert branch materializes DOCX only after legacy decoding, and public legacy read directly expands pieces and parses field state (src/bin/jubarte.rs:3125-3144; src/legacy_doc.rs:180-215,1084-1109,1181-1221,1591-1620). Public scrub::leaks performs bounded ZIP reads but invokes raw XML DOM construction separately (src/scrub.rs:403-469,527-530). These are source-backed availability scenarios retained as findings, without assuming remote deployment.
- Untrusted package bytes cross into the shared engine through admission and OPC parsing. Default agent inspection/editing limits are 64 MiB compressed, 64 MiB per part, 256 MiB total inflated, 10,000 entries and XML depth 256; comparison and rendering use 512 MiB compressed/per part and 2 GiB total inflated, with the same entry/depth defaults. Explicit InputLimitOverrides replace individual base fields and reject unknown keys (src/admission.rs:63-108,137-163; src/inspect.rs:606-615; src/convert/mod.rs:769-778; src/document_comparer.rs:4927).
- ZIP metadata crosses a parser-consistency boundary before normal package processing. Admission checks defects explicitly; the shared normal opener rejects divergent headers, overlapping extents and special entry types. Reads remain in memory rather than extracting document entries to the host filesystem. The debug diagnostic opener intentionally bypasses those structural refusals to inspect rejected packages (src/admission.rs:439-470,516-554; src/opc/archive.rs:15-51).
- Checked XML and permissive DOM parsing are distinct capabilities. Checked APIs apply well-formedness, tag and namespace policy; parse_xdocument deliberately does not enforce input policy, so direct library callers must validate first. Tag defaults include 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte attribute names, 64 MiB attribute values and 128 MiB start tags; other namespace limits also exist (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:44-65,84-114).
- Edit requests cross from caller intent into document mutation. V1 hashes are optional, V2 source hashes mandatory; exact target resolution and shared preparation are separate from filesystem publication. Source hash agreement is a consistency guard, not caller authentication (src/edit.rs:2081-2094; src/edit/v2_apply.rs:74-93; src/edit/prepared.rs:185-230).
- MCP clients invoke document tools through stdio, not an evidenced network listener. --root defaults to startup cwd; the expanded resolved root is opened once as a native OutputRoot. Input paths receive an early containment diagnostic, while actual reads and writes use the held cap-std directory capability. Explicit tool overwrite flags authorize replacing existing outputs; tool annotations are hints rather than an independent authorization mechanism (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:146-199).
- MCP output locations are root/out_dir/clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff, report.json and optional redline.pdf/redline-page-NN.png for editing; rendering uses root/out_dir/page-NN.png and optional render.pdf. OutputRoot validates portable relative paths, stages all files before committing, and commits atomically per file, not as one atomic set (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:161-218,229-255; src/publication.rs:100-143,168-199,228-245).
- Native CLI filesystem publication is explicitly ambient: it opens and holds each selected parent directory and stages outputs. Python CLI and npm CLI have their own host filesystem adapters that read user paths and perform direct writes; those adapters must not inherit MCP confinement or native staged-publication guarantees by implication (src/publication.rs:203-225; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:73-89,101-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-78,106-110).
- Native rendering has an independent ambient font-reading capability. Document font names select installed-font searches; JUBARTE_FONT_DIR overrides platform user font directories. macOS/Unix defaults derive HOME and XDG_DATA_HOME; Windows uses APPDATA for Jubarte fonts and WINDIR/SystemRoot plus LOCALAPPDATA for installed fonts. macOS cloud fonts derive HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts/<family>; the direct family component is checked. Font bytes feed shaping and output generation (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2241-2255,2281-2315,2633-2652,2831-2862).
- Browser WASM accepts byte inputs and returns byte/text results; host filesystem publication is excluded from wasm32, while the Node CLI itself supplies ambient Node filesystem operations. WASM uses the same Rust engine with default features disabled and cli enabled, and its release profile aborts on panic (src/lib.rs:163-165; jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-35,64-69; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:16-21,52-78,106-110).
- Self-update crosses from GitHub release assets into replacement of the running executable. The repository is fixed to jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, sums asset to SHA256SUMS.txt, and binary archive member to jubarte-<version>-<platform>/jubarte[.exe]. It refuses noninteractive install without --yes or --check, checks the selected version, and verifies an extracted regular file. RELEASE_KEYS is currently empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE is None, so present builds consume checksum-only updates; Windows ZIP and Unix tar extraction are delegated to self_update (src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,198-210,240-285,299-320).
- Maintainer publication is separate from runtime document handling. Tag pushes v* trigger release builds with read-only contents permission; only the final release job has contents:write. Optional signing keys are decoded under RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN with exit cleanup and passed to zipsign; release assets and SHA256SUMS.txt are uploaded using a job GITHUB_TOKEN supplied as GH_TOKEN. The local release script separately publishes to crates.io, npm and PyPI, with an explicit version confirmation unless --yes (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-25,51-55,230-307,317-321,356-369; scripts/release.sh:875-899,919-959,1063-1069).
- Native rendering also persists a font index outside the MCP document/output capability. JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides the location (empty/off disables it); otherwise the consumer reads/writes user_font_dir().parent()/font-index.tsv, with temporary extension tsv.<pid>.tmp. Defaults are $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv on macOS, $XDG_DATA_HOME/jubarte/font-index.tsv or $HOME/.local/share/jubarte/font-index.tsv on Linux, and %APPDATA%\jubarte\font-index.tsv on Windows. With custom JUBARTE_FONT_DIR, the index is in that directory's parent. Recipients are the renderer, subsequent renderer processes and local readers permitted by host ACLs. Controls are trusted environment/configuration, delimiter filtering, cached length/mtime checks and write-then-rename; this is ambient std::fs authority, not OutputRoot confinement (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3090-3107,3154-3229,3264-3275).
- Untrusted DOCX crosses ZIP/OPC admission: pre-index budgets, ZIP structural/canonical-name checks, encryption/compression refusal, bounded inflation and XML policy (src/admission.rs:439-569). OPC independently checks archive defects, bounded reads and case-insensitive duplicates (src/opc/archive.rs:34-51; src/opc/mod.rs:282-350,497-524). Rendering admits before normalization/layout (src/convert/mod.rs:769-798).
- Raw XML parse_xdocument intentionally skips input policy; try_parse_xdocument_with_limits validates first. Callers of raw API own prior admission (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:88-135). Tag defaults are 1,024 attributes, 1,024-byte names, 64 MiB values and 128 MiB tags (src/xmllinq/parse.rs:48-65).
- Edit plans cross into mutation using schema/source checks, exact anchors and preparation gates; author labels are not authenticated identities. V1 rejects macro-bearing/signed packages and checks optional source hash; V2 rejects mismatching source hash before operation resolution (src/edit.rs:97-108,2073-2105; src/edit/v2_apply.rs:84-111).
- Native CLI uses ambient operator filesystem authority. publish_paths holds parents, stages/verifies bytes and installs via overwrite rename or no-clobber hard link, atomic per file rather than per set (src/publication.rs:168-174,203-225,419-505,534-570). Python/Node ordinary CLI path writes have different controls (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:76-80,106-111).
- MCP client arguments cross a server-granted directory capability. Startup resolves --root once; diagnostics precede capability-relative actual reads/writes. Portable relative-name checks, overwrite opt-in and output-symlink refusal apply (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1278; src/publication.rs:146-199,228-273,431-463). All connected stdio clients receive registered tools; annotations are hints rather than per-user authorization (mcp_server.py:54-55,126-159,258-277, relative to jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines).
- Native Markdown image loading separately grants --resource-path, otherwise Markdown parent or '.'. Percent decoding precedes scheme/containment checks and capability reads; operator chooses trusted root (src/bin/jubarte.rs:2811-2877,2936-2943). Python/Node reject resource_path; byte conversion defaults to no image callback (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:1057-1064; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:724-727; src/markdown/mod.rs:159-198,226).
- Native font discovery reads system/Word, macOS Office CloudFonts/<family>, and configured/per-user fonts. Cloud family must be a single normal component. Index-named files are read after length/mtime checks; ambient cache persistence is independent of MCP root (src/convert/mod.rs:806-857; src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2258-2315,2637-2652,2855-2862,3154-3188,3213-3227).
- Maintainer debug intentionally uses open_archive_unchecked after budget preflight, reports defects and continues, unlike ordinary refusal paths (src/debug.rs:206-222; src/opc/archive.rs:15-19).
- Native updater downloads fixed repository/platform release assets, checks adjacent checksum and extracted regular-file type, and requires terminal confirmation or --yes; --check does not install. Empty signing keys disable required signatures (src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-125,198-257,299-320).
- Tag CI separates read-only build from contents:write release. Signing-step secret is decoded below RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN and cleaned on exit. Separate GitHub token publishes dist/*; operator script independently publishes Cargo, npm engine/CLI and PyPI (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,54-55,230-290,303-321,356-369; scripts/release.sh:875-919,934-956,1039-1069).
- Contributor pull_request jobs check out contributor code onto self-hosted runners also selected by main pushes; their local action configures a private mbx cache endpoint and documents persistent checkout/store use (.github/workflows/ci.yml:13-16,34-48,72-99; .github/actions/mbx/action.yml:8-12,20-38). External approval and runner isolation controls remain unknown.
- Legacy DOC crosses OLE/FIB/piece decoding then field-block processing before Markdown/DOCX generation; physical chain bounds do not establish logical story or field-work limits (src/legacy_doc.rs:180-215,1090-1225,1596-1619). Embedded WMF crosses binary record decoding to owned text and raster output; MAX_SIDE bounds only pixels (src/convert/metafile.rs:25-31,477-489,603-620,692-700).
- Document supplier to engine process: supplied ZIP/XML/media bytes are untrusted. Admission checks container budgets, names, duplicate identities, encryption/compression and XML policy before higher-level processing; package opens independently apply ZIP header/extent/special-entry consistency checks and bounded package construction. No package extraction to host files is part of this processing. Inspection/edit use default limits; comparison and rendering use the roomier compare policy. Sources: src/admission.rs:13-28,439-495; src/opc/archive.rs:15-51; src/opc/mod.rs:275-284,497-523; src/inspect.rs:604-622; src/convert/mod.rs:769-778.
- Unchecked XML to DOM is a separate library boundary: parse_xdocument is permissive and does not enforce policy. Untrusted XML must pass validate_xml/try_parse_xdocument or admission with active limits first. This is not a schema-validity guarantee. Sources: src/xmllinq/parse.rs:19-36,84-90; SECURITY.md:51-69.
- Edit-plan author to document transformation: plans select anchored operations and may carry a SHA-256 source guard. A guard mismatch returns STALE_SOURCE; omitting the optional guard is supported. This binds requested edits to bytes when present, not to a user identity or externally approved publication. Sources: src/edit.rs:106,2074-2094; SECURITY.md:76-85.
- Native CLI to host filesystem: CLI publication deliberately grants ambient access to caller-selected paths. It uses held parent directory handles, staging and per-file atomic replacement; the complete file set is not atomic. Existing-file replacement requires overwrite/force, and rooted publication has stricter symlink policy than ambient publication. Sources: src/bin/jubarte.rs:261-273; src/publication.rs:168-174,203-225,419-480.
- Python CLI and Node CLI are separate filesystem consumers, not automatically protected by native CLI publication. Python Document.read uses Path.read_bytes, Python CLI _write uses Path.write_bytes, and Node CLI read/write use fs.readFileSync/fs.writeFileSync. They run with their host process's ambient filesystem authority. Sources: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/document.py:141-145; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:116-133; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-110.
- MCP stdio caller to rooted document I/O: the operator chooses an existing resolved root; client paths get early containment diagnostics, but native OutputRoot/cap-std directory-relative reads and publication enforce the actual boundary. Publication validates relative export names, refuses output symlinks and enforces overwrite in the native backend. ToolAnnotations are hints, not the enforcing authorization. The server exposes stdio, without a repository-provided user/session/tenant authentication layer. Sources: jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; jubarte-python/src/lib.rs:1244-1276; src/publication.rs:146-199,228-240,431-463.
- Markdown image references to native CLI filesystem access: --resource-path selects the trusted resource root; otherwise the Markdown file's parent, or '.', is used. URL schemes are refused after percent decoding; canonical containment and an opened OutputRoot capability confine the actual file read. Library image loaders remain caller-supplied capabilities and default to absent. Sources: src/bin/jubarte.rs:2835-2877,2936-2943; src/markdown/mod.rs:159-170.
- Native renderer to host fonts is independent of MCP's document-root capability. The renderer searches configured user fonts, fixed macOS/Word directories, Windows system/user font directories and Word's HOME-relative cloud-font cache. Document font-family names must be a single normal component for direct cloud-folder derivation. Font-index entries can name face files; loading checks recorded metadata before reading them. Index persistence uses ordinary host filesystem operations and a sibling font-index.tsv.<pid>.tmp followed by rename. Sources: src/convert/font.rs:2181-2216,2225-2235,2263-2304,2633-2652,2814-2828,2855-2862,3098-3107,3154-3165,3213-3229.
- Browser/WASM caller to byte APIs: wasm-bindgen passes bytes and explicit options into shared Rust operations; the WASM package omits self-update, and browser/WASM has no host publication dependency. Default comparison inflation allowance exceeds a 32-bit WASM heap, so the host must lower it for its workload. Node CLI filesystem access is host JavaScript authority outside this byte API. Sources: jubarte-wasm/Cargo.toml:18-32; jubarte-wasm/src/lib.rs:77-97; Cargo.toml:150-153; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-79,106-110.
- Operator to native self-updater to release publisher: explicit invocation, check/yes/interactive preflight and optional confirmation gate installation. Repository and binary/archive naming are fixed to jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe], with tar.gz for Unix targets and ZIP support on Windows. The self_update dependency owns retrieval, staging and executable replacement; the application supplies SHA256SUMS.txt verification and a post-extraction regular-file hook. Current signature policy is checksum-only. Sources: src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,120-124,186-257,307-320; Cargo.toml:137-148.
- Tag publisher to release CI: v* tag pushes trigger builds; the release job receives contents:write separately from read-only build jobs. The signing secret, when present, is decoded under RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN and supplied to zipsign for dist/jubarte-*.tar.gz and dist/jubarte-*.zip. The publish step supplies GITHUB_TOKEN as GH_TOKEN to gh release create for github.repository and uploads dist/*. Signing is conditional and currently not required by compiled updater keys. Sources: .github/workflows/release.yml:15-21,51-55,230-307,317-322,356-369.
- PR source to CI runner: main pushes and pull_request events run repository build/lint commands on self-hosted runners, with contents:read and checkout credential persistence disabled. Token scope does not define host isolation; runner lifecycle, filesystem/network isolation, secret residency and GitHub approval settings are not supplied by repository source. Sources: .github/workflows/ci.yml:13-48; .github/workflows/docs.yml:42-48,76-88.
- PR review/remediation workflows to external agents are separate capabilities. Jules PR Review excludes forks and Dependabot, passes JULES_API_KEY plus GITHUB_TOKEN to a pinned external action, grants PR/check write permissions and disables approval. Jules el conejo separately resolves a same-repository PR, checks out the resolved head SHA, and allows only configured trusted actors/authors to start the external AUTO_CREATE_PR session; it passes JULES_API_KEY, starting_branch and require_plan_approval:false. The external action/service implementations and their actual repository-write authorization are absent from this checkout and were not fetched. Sources: .github/workflows/jules-review.yml:18-50; .github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:35-54,65-102,260-324.
- Document producer to shared Rust parser: caller-controlled DOCX/legacy DOC/XML/media enters package admission, OPC handling, DOM construction, editing and rendering. Admission enforces configurable archive and XML limits; WmlDocument first admits under the comparison budget before opening the package. Library unsafe Rust is forbidden, but dependency safety and process resource limits remain separate controls (src/admission.rs:42-98,399-448,486-543; src/wml_document.rs:50-57; src/lib.rs:85-87; SECURITY.md:38-74).
- ZIP central/local metadata to OPC package identity: production open_archive wraps unchecked archive open and validates defects; admission runs the defect pass while preserving typed refusal. Debug inspection intentionally has a different acceptance mode and must not be treated as the production admission boundary (src/opc/archive.rs:20-44,339-364,388-484; src/admission.rs:439-448; SECURITY.md:111-136).
- Raw XML caller to DOM: checked APIs are the intended policy boundary; infallible parse_xdocument deliberately does not enforce input policy. A caller invoking raw low-level APIs owns admission obligations rather than gaining protection solely by linking the crate (SECURITY.md:43-66; src/wml_document.rs:85-96).
- Agent/client to MCP filesystem broker: startup resolves --root, defaulting to cwd, opens _native._OutputRoot once, and serves stdio. Tool paths receive early Python containment checks, but actual reads/publication use the native directory capability; annotation hints are descriptive metadata rather than independent authorization. Every connected caller receives the configured tool capabilities; no per-user ownership or tenant model is established by this source (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:54-55,67-108,373-381; src/publication.rs:146-200).
- MCP edit intent to published artifacts: docx_edit resolves a plan in memory, constructs clean.docx, redline.docx, patch.diff and report.json under caller-selected out_dir inside root, optionally adds rendered artifacts, then calls the broker's publish operation. Invalid plans are reported before publication; actual filesystem publication is atomic per file, not across the output set, and errors identify already committed files (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:153-218; src/publication.rs:83-143,168-199). No human approval boundary is implemented or claimed here.
- Local CLI caller to ambient host publication: native publish_paths grants explicit local path authority, while Node's wrapper directly reads/writes paths with fs and checks overwrite separately. These interfaces do not inherit MCP's root confinement merely because the engine/parser is shared (src/publication.rs:203-225; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:52-78,106-111,137-153).
- Document font family/native rendering to host font resources: installed_family_faces obtains the environment-derived user font folder and Word cloud-font root and scans known font directories. Cloud family names must be one normal path component; cached index entries can subsequently name font files directly. Persistent font-index loading/saving and font reads operate with ambient process authority (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2235,2258-2315,2633-2652,3090-3107,3154-3171,3182-3229). This boundary exists during native MCP rendering even when document paths are confined.
- Local operator to executable update: self-update checks interactivity before network lookup, chooses one of five OS/architecture targets, obtains the project's GitHub release, requires explicit intent for downgrades and confirmation unless --yes, downloads the target with SHA256SUMS.txt verification, registers a regular-file extracted-binary hook and replaces the executable through self_update. Current source has no trusted signing key, so GitHub release-asset integrity remains part of the trust chain (src/update.rs:25-48,83-109,120-125,144-161,186-258,299-307).
- Maintainer/tag authority to public release artifacts: v* pushes trigger builds; tag and Cargo version must match; binary jobs have contents:read; signing consumes a step-scoped private-key secret and checks archives against committed public keys when present. Release publication consumes a separate GitHub token. Actual repository protection, environment approvals and external registry/account configuration are not established by these workflow declarations (.github/workflows/release.yml:15-49,51-74,261-309,356-369).
- Same-repository PR content to external Jules reviewer: the review workflow excludes fork/Dependabot PRs, performs no local checkout, delegates to a SHA-pinned external action, grants read content plus write PR/check permissions, and disables model approval. External action/Jules implementation is absent from inspected repository source, so its downstream session controls and secret handling remain unresolved (.github/workflows/jules-review.yml:17-50). The separate el-conejo workflow resolves a same-repository PR and checks out its exact head with persisted credentials disabled; it has its own author/dispatcher trust configuration and should not be collapsed into the first workflow's authority (.github/workflows/jules-el-conejo-review.yml:35-52,58-102).
- Assigned discovery-0026 architecture (supplied Standard context): Untrusted package bytes enter ZIP/XML/DOM and image/font/legacy parsers. Admission checks budgets, header/extent consistency, safe canonical names, duplicate identities, encryption/compression and bounded inflation. Checked XML validates before DOM construction; permissive parse_xdocument deliberately does not enforce policy (src/admission.rs:439-575; src/xmllinq/parse.rs:90-135). Renderer first admits input, normalizes Strict packages, opens PartFs and expands package-contained altChunks (src/convert/mod.rs:769-808).
- Assigned discovery-0026 architecture (supplied Standard context): JSON edit requests cross into mutation: preparation resolves/checks operations and candidate results before returning bytes; legacy SHA-256 guarding is optional, not authentication or approval. Signed/macro-bearing edit packages are refused (src/edit/prepared.rs:196-235; src/edit.rs:2081-2105).
- Assigned discovery-0026 architecture (supplied Standard context): MCP clients share operator --root, default cwd. Startup normalizes root and opens _OutputRoot; actual reads/publication use retained cap-std authority, not merely Python resolve checks. Writes validate relative names, reject output symlinks and require overwrite for conflicts. No tenant separation or human approval is implemented (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:67-108,373-381; src/publication.rs:146-199,228-250,431-463).
- Assigned discovery-0026 architecture (supplied Standard context): Native CLI publication grants ambient selected-path authority; Python/Node CLI handlers also own ambient host I/O and do not inherit MCP confinement.
