The policy's dependency identity is stale; this discrepancy alone establishes no vulnerability.
- Per-file atomic publication does not guarantee all-or-nothing multi-file filesystem commits. OutputRoot stages the complete set, but later commit or durability failures can report already committed files (src/publication.rs:100-143,168-174).
- Process memory/CPU isolation is a host obligation explicitly recommended by SECURITY.md, not an engine-enforced sandbox. Runtime resource ceilings outside admission and raster checks remain deployment dependent.
- Legacy DOC parsing is an additional OLE/compound-file input path, not a DOCX ZIP path. Its parser performs structural sector/chain checks, but this pass did not map a complete equivalent admission budget (src/legacy_doc.rs:180-181,657-740).
- No application execution, external service contact, mutation or vulnerability-triggering input was used. Dependency implementations, generated WASM implementation parity, exhaustive parser coverage and actual remote account/registry configuration remain outside this focused architecture verification.
- Font-index temporary writes use a predictable PID sibling and follow symlinks; exploitation needs another actor able to alter the configured cache parent. Default locations are under user data directories, and no shared writable cache deployment was provided. Document family names select lookup keys, not the configured cache destination (src/convert/font.rs:3090-3107,3213-3229).
- Read-only architecture/static inspection; no application execution, external-service checks or runtime reproduction. Input budgets are not a proof against all DoS; hosts remain responsible for process limits (SECURITY.md:70-74).
- MCP says tools only touch files under root (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:7-9,67-68), but render calls native installed-font discovery and index persistence outside that root (same file:234-236; src/convert/mod.rs:857; src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3100-3106,3183-3188,3213-3227). Tool document/output I/O is confined; broad documentation guarantee is narrower in implementation. No exploit established merely from font use.
- Default font index is parent(JUBARTE_FONT_DIR)/font-index.tsv when overridden; otherwise macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Linux ${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Windows %APPDATA%/jubarte/font-index.tsv. JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX overrides full path; empty/off disables. It is not inside fonts nor necessarily MCP root; temporary sibling is font-index.tsv.<PID>.tmp (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,3100-3106,3213-3227).
- Font discovery: macOS System/Library/Fonts[/Supplemental], /Library/Fonts[/Microsoft], Word.app/Contents/Resources/DFonts, $HOME/Library/Group Containers/UBF8T346G9.Office/FontCache/4/CloudFonts/<family>, plus configured/per-user Jubarte fonts (src/convert/font.rs:2225-2236,2281-2315,2637-2652). Windows uses WINDIR then SystemRoot then C:\Windows, /Fonts, optional LOCALAPPDATA/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts, and JUBARTE_FONT_DIR or APPDATA/jubarte/fonts (src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652,2831-2862). Linux uses JUBARTE_FONT_DIR or ${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/jubarte/fonts; absent required HOME removes default (2637-2652). Host permissions enforce these reads; not MCP root.
- Native publication effective staging is <destination-parent>/.jubarte-<UUID>.partial/bytes with held parent, exclusive creation/readback and atomic rename/hard-link. MCP edit outputs are <root>/<out_dir>/{clean.docx,redline.docx,patch.diff,report.json,optional redline.pdf/redline-page-NN.png}; MCP render outputs page-NN.png and optional render.pdf, with client receiving reports/text (src/publication.rs:419-570; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:185-255).
- Updater effective assets are jubarte-<version>-{macos,linux}-{aarch64,x86_64}.tar.gz or windows-x86_64.zip; internal jubarte-<version>-<target>/jubarte[.exe] replaces installed executable. Fixed repository is jandira-tech/jubarte-redlines, current control TLS/checksum/regular-file check/confirmation. RELEASE_KEYS empty and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE None intentionally mean checksum-only; actual CI key-secret availability unknown (src/update.rs:25-48,91-108,198-285; docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:59-80; .github/workflows/release.yml:268-275).
- SELF_UPDATE says GH_TOKEN/GITHUB_TOKEN lift rate limits (docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:22-24), but updater never opts into environment-token loading (src/update.rs:198-211). Architecture reviewer inspected locked self_update 1.3.1 default auth_token=None/auth_token_from_env=false and token copying; supporting cached dependency src/backends/common.rs:1027-1053,1077-1085 and src/macros.rs:580; repository Cargo.lock:2552-2553. Documentation/availability discrepancy, not evidence of leakage.
- Frontend common flags do not establish identical filesystem controls: Python/Node ordinary output uses host path APIs and rejects unsupported capabilities; WASM has no native OutputRoot module/dependency, Node filesystem belongs to JS host (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/__main__.py:101-133,1048-1073; jubarte-wasm/cli/bin/jubarte-redlines.mjs:6-14,76-80,106-111,724-740; Cargo.toml:173-177; src/lib.rs:160-165).
- CI signing receives only ZIPSIGN_PRIVATE_KEY in the signing shell/zipsign process, stores decoded keys under RUNNER_TEMP/zipsign.<random>/keyN, validates 64-byte keys, cleans on exit and signs dist/jubarte-*.tar.gz/zip; verification uses committed keys when present. Release publisher independently uses secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN as GH_TOKEN with contents:write, github.repository and GITHUB_REF_NAME to publish notes.md/dist/* (.github/workflows/release.yml:230-321,356-369).

Secret scopes and actual provider accounts are external.

- Documentation says every MCP path/tool only touches files under --root and Python Document operations write no output files. Actual brokered document I/O uses OutputRoot, but rendering also reads ambient font directories and writes an ambient per-user font index. Therefore the broad root-only wording must not be interpreted as process-wide filesystem confinement (jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:5-11,67-108,234-255; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/document.py:116-123; src/convert/font.rs:2633-2652,3090-3107,3182-3229).
- Documentation says core operations use complete bytes with no temporary files. Native font-index persistence creates a sibling PID-suffixed .tmp file before renaming, and host publication deliberately creates staging. The byte-level document-processing statement and incidental host filesystem behavior must both remain visible; this architecture difference is not by itself a vulnerability (src/lib.rs:7-11; src/convert/font.rs:3221-3229; src/publication.rs:284-312).
- Self-update documentation describes staged signing and correctly says empty embedded keys mean checksum-only operation. Actual RELEASE_KEYS=&[] and FIRST_SIGNED_RELEASE=None confirm that signing support is currently inactive in the updater. Whether GitHub's private signing secret exists is not inferable from source (docs/SELF_UPDATE.md:59-87; src/update.rs:44-48; .github/workflows/release.yml:261-275).
- SECURITY.md describes rdocx-opc 0.2 and quick-xml 0.41 remediation. Current manifest instead uses oxml-opc 0.14.2, quick-xml 0.42 for checked admission and an explicitly aliased 0.41 for OPC error compatibility. Preserve this documentation/dependency-name discrepancy without inferring either an exploit or lack of patched behavior (SECURITY.md:89-97; Cargo.toml:102-117; src/opc/mod.rs:15-17,161-164).
- Default and comparison admission budgets are materially different. WmlDocument admits under the roomier comparison budget, so a general statement that the default 64 MiB/256 MiB budget protects every public use would be inaccurate (src/admission.rs:25-27,63-98; src/wml_document.rs:50-57).
- Output publication is atomic per file rather than transactional across an output set, and it reports partial commits. Python plan-level atomicity claims must not be generalized into rollback of successful filesystem commits (src/publication.rs:5-7,36-46,83-143,168-174; jubarte-python/python/jubarte_redlines/mcp_server.py:161-168,217).
- Native font-cache locations depend on operator environment, cwd when overrides are relative, and platform. Evidence: src/convert/font.rs:2637-2652. Default index paths are macOS $HOME/Library/Application Support/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Linux ${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/jubarte/font-index.tsv, Windows %APPDATA%\jubarte\font-index.tsv; a JUBARTE_FONT_DIR override relocates the index to its parent; JUBARTE_FONT_INDEX empty/off disables it.
